电路分析基础 · 教材逐题Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis · Textbook exercise library

全书360道主习题清单，含范围外章节；现有43道完整题干与核验解析。计数按主习题，不把小问另算一题。360 numbered exercises across the full book, including chapters outside exam coverage. 43 currently have complete statements and checked solutions. Subparts are not counted as separate main exercises.

“清单已核对”只表示题号与来源页已收录，不表示题干已抄录或答案已完成。部分章的范围需要逐题确认。“Inventory checked” means that an ID and source page are indexed; it does not mean the statement or solution is complete. Coverage in partially taught chapters needs individual mapping.

1-1全书习题Book exercise已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第32页／PDF第41页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 32 / PDF p. 41. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

参考方向与实际方向：用正负号判断Reference and actual directions: interpret the sign

题目原文Transcribed question

已知在如题图1-1所示电路的电压、电流参考方向下，u=5 V，i=−2 A，请标出电压、电流的实际方向。With the voltage and current references in Fig. 1-1, u = 5 V and i = −2 A. Mark the actual voltage and current directions.

元件左端a、右端b；电流i参考箭头由a指向b；电压u左正右负。A two-terminal element has a at the left and b at the right. The reference current points from a to b, and voltage u is positive at a.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

参考方向是预先指定的坐标；正值表示与参考相同，负值表示相反。A reference is a chosen coordinate direction: a positive value agrees with it; a negative value reverses it.
这类题不需要另列电路方程。先把u解释为Vₐ−Vᵦ，再把i的负号解释为箭头反向。No additional circuit equations are needed. Interpret u as Vₐ−Vᵦ, then reverse the current arrow because i is negative.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

判断电压高低Determine the higher potential

u=Vₐ−Vᵦ=5 V>0，所以a端比b端高5 V；电压降的实际方向为a→b，即左高右低。u = Vₐ−Vᵦ = 5 V > 0, so a is 5 V above b. The actual voltage-drop direction is a→b.

u=Va−Vb=5V

反转负电流的箭头Reverse the negative current

i的参考方向为a→b，但i=−2 A，故实际电流为2 A，方向b→a。解答示意图给出了两个实际方向。The current reference is a→b, but i = −2 A. The actual current is therefore 2 A from b to a. The solution diagram shows both actual directions.

ia→b=−2A ⟺ ib→a=2A

实际方向示意：左端a电位较高、右端b较低，5 V电压降向右；实际2 A电流向左，由b流向a。Actual directions: a at left is higher than b at right. The 5 V voltage drop points right; actual 2 A current points left, from b to a.

电压：a高、b低，实际电压降a→b；电流：2 A，由b→a。Voltage: a is higher and b lower, with a→b voltage drop. Current: 2 A from b→a.

易错点Common errors

  • 负电流不是“没有电流”，而是与选定参考箭头相反。A negative current is not an absent current; it flows opposite the chosen reference arrow.
  • 电压的方向表示高低电位；不要把它误当作一定与电流同向。Voltage direction describes potential order and need not match the current direction.

核验依据Verification

独立参考反转核验：令Vₐ=5 V、Vᵦ=0，则Vₐ−Vᵦ=5 V；把电流坐标改为b→a，数值由−2变成+2 A。与书第332页/PDF341页1-1一致。Independent reference-reversal check: Vₐ = 5 V and Vᵦ = 0 reproduce u = 5 V. Reversing the current coordinate to b→a changes −2 A into +2 A. This matches answer 1-1 on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341.

1-2全书习题Book exercise已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第32页／PDF第41页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 32 / PDF p. 41. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

电荷求电流：正弦函数求导Current from charge: differentiate the sinusoid

题目原文Transcribed question

已知通过某二端元件的电荷q(t)=2sin(3t) C，t>0，求电流i(t)。The charge through a two-terminal element is q(t) = 2sin(3t) C for t > 0. Find i(t).

原书1-2完整题干，电荷为2sin(3t) C，时间范围t>0。The complete original 1-2 statement: q(t) = 2sin(3t) C for t > 0.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

电流定义i=dq/dt；复合函数求导；C/s=A。Current definition i = dq/dt, the chain rule and C/s = A.
给的是电荷而不是电流。求电流只需对时间求导，正弦求导后的系数还要乘以内部的3。The given quantity is charge. Differentiate it with respect to time, including the inner factor 3.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

写出定义Apply the definition

电流是沿同一参考方向的电荷变化率，单位为库仑每秒。Current is the rate of charge change in the same reference direction, measured in coulombs per second.

i(t)=dq(t)dt

应用链式法则Apply the chain rule

2sin(3t)求导后是2×3cos(3t)，其中3来自3t对t的导数。Differentiating 2sin(3t) gives 2×3cos(3t), where 3 is the derivative of the inner argument 3t.

i(t)=2·3cos(3t)=6cos(3t)A,t>0

i(t)=6cos(3t) A，t>0。i(t) = 6cos(3t) A, t > 0.

易错点Common errors

  • 不要漏掉链式法则中的3。Do not omit the chain-rule factor 3.
  • q是有符号的累计电荷，不要求在所有时刻为正。q is signed accumulated charge; it need not be positive at every instant.

核验依据Verification

独立积分核验：∫₀ᵗ6cos(3τ)dτ=2sin(3t)=q(t)−q(0)。另在多个时间点对原始q做中心差分，与6cos(3t)相符，误差小于10⁻⁸ A。与书第332页/PDF341页1-2一致。Independent integral check: ∫₀ᵗ6cos(3τ)dτ = 2sin(3t) = q(t)−q(0). Central differences of the original q at multiple times agree with 6cos(3t) to less than 10⁻⁸ A. This matches answer 1-2 on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341.

1-3全书习题Book exercise已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第32页／PDF第41页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 32 / PDF p. 41. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

指数电荷求导：常数消失，指数不变Exponential charge: constants vanish and the exponent remains

题目原文Transcribed question

已知通过某二端元件的电荷q(t)=(10+5e^(−3t)) C，t>0，求通过此元件的电流。The charge through a two-terminal element is q(t) = (10+5e^(−3t)) C for t > 0. Find the current through it.

原书1-3完整题干，指数清楚为−3t；q(t)=(10+5e^(−3t)) C。The complete original 1-3 statement, clearly showing exponent −3t in q(t) = (10+5e^(−3t)) C.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

电流i=dq/dt；指数函数链式法则；电荷守恒。i = dq/dt, the chain rule for exponentials and charge conservation.
10 C是常数项，其导数为0；e^(−3t)求导只增加系数−3，不会把原指数改成别的数。The constant 10 C differentiates to zero. Differentiating e^(−3t) multiplies it by −3 without changing its exponent.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

对原题逐项求导Differentiate the original statement term by term

严格采用题干指数−3t：常数10的导数为0，5e^(−3t)的导数为−15e^(−3t)。Use the original exponent −3t. The derivative of 10 is zero and that of 5e^(−3t) is −15e^(−3t).

i(t)=ddt(10+5e−3t)=−15e−3tA

用总电荷变化排除错误指数Reject a wrong exponent using total charge change

q(0⁺)=15 C，q(∞)=10 C，所以从0到∞的总电流积分必须为−5 C。正确式积分为−15/3=−5 C；附录−15e^(−5t)的积分仅−3 C，不能还原原题。q(0⁺) = 15 C and q(∞) = 10 C, so total integrated current must be −5 C. The correct expression integrates to −15/3 = −5 C; the appendix expression −15e^(−5t) integrates to only −3 C and fails to reconstruct the given charge.

∫0∞i(t)dt=−153=−5C=q(∞)−q(0+)

i(t)=−15e^(−3t) A，t>0。书后答案的指数−5t与原题不一致，应按题干−3t。i(t) = −15e^(−3t) A for t > 0. The appendix exponent −5t conflicts with the original statement; use −3t.

易错点Common errors

  • 求导会改变前面的系数，不会把e^(−3t)改为e^(−5t)。Differentiation changes the coefficient, not e^(−3t) into e^(−5t).
  • 不能仅因书后印有答案就覆盖原题和积分守恒。Do not override the original statement and charge balance solely because an appendix answer is printed.

核验依据Verification

独立双重核验：对原q做多点中心差分，误差小于10⁻⁸ A；正确电流从0到∞的积分为−5 C，与15 C降到10 C完全相符。高分辨率核对题干第32页/PDF41页为−3t，附录第332页/PDF341页印为−5t，确认为附录指数错误。Two independent checks: central differences of the original q agree to less than 10⁻⁸ A, and integrating current to infinity gives −5 C, exactly the fall from 15 C to 10 C. High-resolution inspection confirms −3t in the statement on printed p. 32/PDF p. 41 but −5t in the appendix on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341; the appendix exponent is wrong.

1-4全书习题Book exercise已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第32页／PDF第41页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 32 / PDF p. 41. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

电荷波形到电流与功率：斜率和能量符号From charge to current and power: slopes and energy signs

题目原文Transcribed question

设通过题图1-2(a)所示元件的电荷波形如题图1-2(b)所示。若单位正电荷由a端移至b端时获得的能量为2 J，求流过元件的电流i(t)及元件的功率p(t)，并画出i(t)与p(t)的波形。The charge waveform through the element in Fig. 1-2(a) is shown in Fig. 1-2(b). A unit positive charge gains 2 J when moving from terminal a to b. Find current i(t) and element power p(t), and draw both waveforms.

a在左、b在右，i参考方向a→b，u左正右负。q由0到1 s按5t² C增加到5 C；1到2 s保持5 C；2到3 s线性降至0。a is left, b right, reference i is a→b and u is positive at a. q rises as 5t² C from 0 to 1 s, stays at 5 C from 1 to 2 s, then falls linearly to 0 at 3 s.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

i=dq/dt；电压为单位正电荷的电位能差；关联方向下p吸收=ui；分段函数单侧极限。i = dq/dt, voltage as potential-energy difference per unit charge, absorbed power p = ui under passive references, and one-sided limits of piecewise functions.
先从“正电荷获得能量”判断b比a电位高，得到u=−2 V；再逐段读取q的斜率求i，最后按原图关联参考方向相乘得到吸收功率。First interpret the gained energy: b is above a in potential, so u = −2 V. Then differentiate each charge segment and multiply by u using the original passive references.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

把获得的能量换成图示电压Convert gained energy into the referenced voltage

单位正电荷从a到b获得2 J，故Vᵦ−Vₐ=2 V；图示u=Vₐ−Vᵦ，所以u=−2 V。A unit positive charge gains 2 J from a to b, so Vᵦ−Vₐ = 2 V. The drawn voltage is u = Vₐ−Vᵦ = −2 V.

u=Va−Vb=−2V

分段写q并求导Write and differentiate the charge pieces

0<t<1时q=5t²；1<t<2时q=5；2<t<3时q=15−5t。对应斜率分别为10t、0、−5，单位A。For 0<t<1, q = 5t²; for 1<t<2, q = 5; for 2<t<3, q = 15−5t. Their slopes are 10t, 0 and −5 A.

i(t)={10tA,0<t<10,1<t<2−5A,2<t<3

求吸收功率并画波形Compute absorbed power and draw the waveforms

i从u正端流入，因此p=ui=−2i。第一段负功率表示元件提供能量；第三段正功率表示吸收能量。解答图中的虚线只提示跳变，不代表额外冲激。Current enters u’s positive terminal, so p = ui = −2i. Negative first-segment power means energy delivery; positive third-segment power means absorption. Dashed jump guides in the solution plot do not represent impulses.

pabs(t)={−20tW,0<t<10,1<t<210W,2<t<3

说明分段交界和图外延拓State the corner and extension conventions

在t=1、2、3处，q的斜率发生跳变，通常不为导数单独指定一个值，应按左右极限画图。q本身连续，因此电流没有冲激。若按图中3 s后的零值水平线延拓，则t>3时i=p=0。At t = 1, 2 and 3 the charge slope jumps, so use one-sided limits rather than assigning a unique derivative value. Charge is continuous, hence no current impulse. If the zero-charge horizontal continuation after 3 s is used, i = p = 0 for t > 3.

两幅精确分段波形：i从0线性升到10 A的左极限，在1 s降到0，2 s降到−5 A，3 s回0；p从0线性降到−20 W的左极限，在1 s回0，2 s升到10 W，3 s回0。虚线为跳变提示，开圆为单侧极限。Exact piecewise plots: i rises linearly from 0 to the 10 A left limit, drops to 0 at 1 s, then to −5 A at 2 s and returns to 0 at 3 s. p falls linearly to the −20 W left limit, returns to 0 at 1 s, rises to 10 W at 2 s and returns to 0 at 3 s. Dashed lines guide jumps; open circles show one-sided limits.

u=−2 V。i：0<t<1为10t A，1<t<2为0，2<t<3为−5 A；p吸收依次为−20t W、0、10 W。波形见解答图；交界点用单侧极限。u = −2 V. i is 10t A for 0<t<1, 0 for 1<t<2 and −5 A for 2<t<3. Absorbed power is respectively −20t W, 0 and 10 W. See the solution plots; use one-sided limits at corners.

易错点Common errors

  • “电荷获得能量”给出Vᵦ−Vₐ>0，图示u却是Vₐ−Vᵦ。The gained energy implies Vᵦ−Vₐ > 0, whereas the drawn u is Vₐ−Vᵦ.
  • q的恒定平台对应零电流，不是5 A。A constant charge plateau means zero current, not 5 A.
  • q连续但斜率跳变，只产生电流跳变，不产生电流冲激。Continuous q with a slope jump creates a current jump, not an impulse.

核验依据Verification

独立电荷/能量核验：三段电流积分为+5、0、−5 C，总计0，重建q由0回到0；吸收能量为−10、0、+10 J，总计0，等于uΔq。各非交界时间点对原q作差分也相符。附录第332页/PDF341页列出的各段电流、功率与此一致。Independent charge/energy checks: integrated segment currents are +5, 0 and −5 C, totaling zero and reconstructing the return of q to zero. Absorbed energies are −10, 0 and +10 J, totaling uΔq = 0. Differentiation checks away from corners also agree. The appendix segment currents and powers on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341 match.

1-5指定作业Assigned已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第32页／PDF第41页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 32 / PDF p. 41. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：1-5Assigned requirement: 1-5

参考方向与吸收功率Reference directions and absorbed power

题目原文Transcribed question

已知在题图1-3所示电压、电流参考方向下，元件A的电压、电流分别为u=-3 V、i=5 A，元件B的电压、电流分别为u=5 V、i=-2 mA，试求两元件吸收的功率。Under the voltage and current reference directions shown in Fig. 1-3, element A has u = -3 V and i = 5 A, while element B has u = 5 V and i = -2 mA. Find the power absorbed by each element.

A：电流参考方向由左向右，电压左正右负；B：电流参考方向由右向左，电压右正左负。两者电流箭头都流入电压正参考端。A: current points left to right, with voltage positive on the left. B: current points right to left, with voltage positive on the right. Both current references enter the positive voltage-reference terminal.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

电压和电流参考方向；关联参考方向；功率符号与单位换算。Voltage and current reference directions; passive sign convention; power signs and unit conversion.
先看箭头是否流入标“+”的端子，再把带符号的数值代入公式。First check whether the current arrow enters the terminal marked +, then substitute the signed values.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

确认参考方向Check the reference directions

A和B的电流参考方向都流入电压正参考端，因此两者吸收功率公式均为p=ui。For both A and B, the current reference enters the positive voltage-reference terminal, so absorbed power is p = ui.

pabs=ui

计算ACalculate A

A的电压为负，按给定参考方向直接代入。负吸收功率表示该元件实际提供功率。Substitute the signed voltage for A. A negative absorbed power means that the element delivers power.

pA=(−3)(5)=−15W

计算B并统一单位Calculate B and convert units

把-2 mA换成-0.002 A。B的吸收功率为-0.010 W，即-10 mW。Convert -2 mA to -0.002 A. B absorbs -0.010 W, or -10 mW.

pB=5(−2×10−3)=−0.010W=−10mW

A吸收-15 W（提供15 W）；B吸收-10 mW（提供10 mW）。A absorbs -15 W (delivers 15 W); B absorbs -10 mW (delivers 10 mW).

易错点Common errors

  • 不要因i为负而翻转箭头后仍代入原负值，避免重复改符号。Do not reverse the arrow for a negative current and then keep the original negative value; that would change the sign twice.
  • mA与A相差1000倍。mA and A differ by a factor of 1000.

核验依据Verification

按关联参考方向独立计算，并与书第332页（PDF341页）1-5参考答案逐项一致。Independently calculated using the passive sign convention and matched item by item to answer 1-5 on printed p. 332 (PDF p. 341).

1-6全书习题Book exercise已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第32页／PDF第41页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 32 / PDF p. 41. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

局部电路KCL：允许未知电流取负Local KCL: allow a negative unknown current

题目原文Transcribed question

求题图1-4所示部分电路中的电流i₁和i₂。Find currents i₁ and i₂ in the partial circuit of Fig. 1-4.

左节点2 A流入、5 A流出；4 A源从左到右；下方电阻i₁参考向右。右节点8 A流出、6 A流入；i₂参考从右下方流入。At the left node 2 A enters and 5 A leaves. A 4 A source points left to right, as does bottom reference i₁. At the right node 8 A leaves and 6 A enters; i₂ enters from the lower right.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

基尔霍夫电流定律；按参考箭头代数求和；整体边界电流守恒。Kirchhoff’s current law, algebraic currents in their reference directions and boundary-current conservation.
每个节点写一条“流入=流出”。未知量先按题目箭头保留，求出负值后再解释实际方向。Write entering = leaving at each node. Keep each unknown in the given reference, then interpret its actual direction after solving.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

左节点求i₁Use the left node for i₁

2 A流入；5 A、4 A和参考i₁流出，所以2=5+4+i₁。2 A enters; 5 A, 4 A and reference i₁ leave, giving 2 = 5+4+i₁.

2=5+4+i1⇒i1=−7A

右节点求i₂Use the right node for i₂

4 A、参考i₁、6 A及i₂流入，8 A流出。代入i₁=−7 A，得到i₂=5 A。4 A, reference i₁, 6 A and i₂ enter; 8 A leaves. Substituting i₁ = −7 A gives i₂ = 5 A.

4+i1+6+i2=8⇒i2=5A

i₁=−7 A（实际由右向左7 A）；i₂=5 A（按原图从右下方流入右节点）。i₁ = −7 A, actually 7 A right to left. i₂ = 5 A, entering the right node from the lower right as drawn.

易错点Common errors

  • i₁在右节点参考流入，代入时必须保留−7 A，不能改成+7而不改箭头。At the right node i₁ is referenced inward; retain −7 A unless you also reverse its arrow.
  • 4 A是连接两个节点的内部电流，整体边界KCL中会相消。The 4 A source is internal to the two-node region and cancels in the boundary KCL.

核验依据Verification

独立整体边界核验：外部总流入2+6+5=13 A，总流出5+8=13 A；内部4 A和i₁均相消。另独立联立两节点方程得到同样的−7、5 A。与附录第332页/PDF341页1-6一致。Independent boundary check: external inflow 2+6+5 = 13 A equals outflow 5+8 = 13 A; internal 4 A and i₁ cancel. An independent simultaneous solve of both node equations gives −7 and 5 A. This matches appendix 1-6 on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341.

1-7指定作业Assigned已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第32页／PDF第41页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 32 / PDF p. 41. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：1-7Assigned requirement: 1-7

已知支路电流，结合KCL与KVL求未知电流Use KCL and KVL with known branch currents

题目原文Transcribed question

题图1-5所示电路为某电路的一部分，已知i₁=6 A，i₂=-2 A，求图中电流i。The circuit in Fig. 1-5 is part of a larger circuit. Given i₁ = 6 A and i₂ = -2 A, find the current i shown in the figure.

三个左端口分别经3 Ω电阻接入上、中、下节点，i₁、i₂、i₃参考方向均向右。上、中节点间6 Ω，中、下节点间18 Ω，上、下节点间右支路12 Ω；待求i沿12 Ω向下。Three left ports connect through 3 Ω resistors to the upper, middle, and lower nodes; i₁, i₂, and i₃ all point right. There are 6 Ω between upper and middle nodes, 18 Ω between middle and lower nodes, and 12 Ω in the right branch from upper to lower, with unknown i pointing downward.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

KCL、KVL、电阻关联参考方向。KCL, KVL, and the resistor passive sign convention.
已有两个注入电流，先用KCL把内部支路电流都写成i，再对两条上到下的路径列一次KVL；不用求三个左端口的电压。With two injection currents already known, use KCL to express the internal branch currents in terms of i, then equate the voltage drops along the two upper-to-lower paths. The left-port voltages are unnecessary.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

上节点列KCLApply KCL at the upper node

设6 Ω中的电流向下为i₆。流入的6 A分成i和i₆，所以i₆=6-i。Let i₆ point downward through 6 Ω. The 6 A entering the upper node splits into i and i₆.

i6=6−i

中节点列KCLApply KCL at the middle node

设18 Ω中的电流向下为i₁₈。i₂=-2 A已经包含参考方向的信息，因此i₁₈=i₆+i₂=4-i。Let i₁₈ point downward through 18 Ω. The value i₂ = -2 A already includes its reference-direction sign, so i₁₈ = i₆ + i₂ = 4 - i.

i18=i6+i2=4−i

同起点、同终点的电压相等Equate voltage drops between the same nodes

上到下经12 Ω的电压降为12i；经6 Ω和18 Ω的总电压降为6(6-i)+18(4-i)。The voltage drop from upper to lower is 12i through 12 Ω and 6(6-i) + 18(4-i) through the other path.

12i=6(6−i)+18(4−i)

解方程并解释方向Solve and interpret the direction

整理得36i=108，故i=3 A。正号表示实际方向与图中向下的箭头一致。Rearranging gives 36i = 108, so i = 3 A. The positive result agrees with the downward arrow.

i=3A

i=3 A，沿12 Ω电阻向下。i = 3 A, downward through the 12 Ω resistor.

易错点Common errors

  • i₂=-2 A表示中间端口实际流出2 A，KCL中保留负号。i₂ = -2 A means 2 A actually leaves the middle port; retain the negative sign in KCL.
  • 三个3 Ω电阻的电流已给定或可由整体KCL确定，求i不需要先算它们的压降。The port currents are given or follow from overall KCL; their 3 Ω voltage drops are unnecessary for finding i.

核验依据Verification

第二种方法：以下节点为0 V独立列节点方程，得上节点36 V、中节点18 V，故i=36/12=3 A。内部电流i₆=3 A、i₁₈=1 A满足全部KCL；书第332页（PDF341页）答案亦为3 A。Independent nodal check with the lower node at 0 V gives upper 36 V and middle 18 V, hence i = 36/12 = 3 A. Internal currents i₆ = 3 A and i₁₈ = 1 A satisfy every KCL equation. The answer on printed p. 332 (PDF p. 341) is also 3 A.

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第33页／PDF第42页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 33 / PDF p. 42. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

复杂极性图：先赋节点电位A polarity-heavy figure: assign node potentials first

题目原文Transcribed question

求题图1-6所示电路中的电压u₁、u₂和u₃。Find voltages u₁, u₂ and u₃ in Fig. 1-6.

菱形节点T顶、B底、L左、R右。外10 V源T正B负；左下10 V为L正B负；右下6 V为R正B负。u₁为L正R负，u₂为R正T负，u₃为T正L负。Diamond nodes are T top, B bottom, L left and R right. Outer 10 V is positive at T relative to B; lower-left 10 V at L relative to B; lower-right 6 V at R relative to B. u₁ = V_L−V_R, u₂ = V_R−V_T and u₃ = V_T−V_L.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

节点电位；电压等于正端电位减负端电位；KVL。Node potentials, voltage as positive-terminal potential minus negative-terminal potential, and KVL.
将底节点设为0 V，可直接读出T、L、R三个电位。随后所有未知电压只需按图示极性相减，避免在斜边上反复判断符号。Set the bottom node to zero and directly read T, L and R. Obtain each unknown by subtracting the negative-terminal potential from the positive-terminal potential.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

选择参考节点Choose a reference node

令V_B=0。三个已知电压分别给出V_T=10 V、V_L=10 V、V_R=6 V。Choose V_B = 0. The given voltages yield V_T = 10 V, V_L = 10 V and V_R = 6 V.

VB=0,VT=10,VL=10,VR=6(V)

按三个未知量的正负端相减Subtract using each unknown polarity

u₁左正右负；u₂右节点为正、顶节点为负；u₃顶节点为正、左节点为负。u₁ is positive at L and negative at R. u₂ is positive at R and negative at T. u₃ is positive at T and negative at L.

u1=VL−VR=4V,u2=VR−VT=−4V,u3=VT−VL=0

u₁=4 V，u₂=−4 V，u₃=0 V。u₁ = 4 V, u₂ = −4 V and u₃ = 0 V.

易错点Common errors

  • 原图斜向正号旋转后外形近似×，应结合两端极性看清位置。The rotated plus signs resemble ×; identify which end of each diagonal branch is positive.
  • 两端都有10 V对地电位时，端间电压是0，不是20 V。Two terminals each at 10 V relative to ground have zero voltage between them, not 20 V.

核验依据Verification

独立KVL核验：中央三边环路u₁+u₂+u₃=4−4+0=0。节点差值同时重现外10 V、左下10 V及右下6 V三个已知量。与附录第332页/PDF341页1-8一致。Independent KVL check: u₁+u₂+u₃ = 4−4+0 = 0 around the central triangle. The potentials also reproduce all three given 10 V, 10 V and 6 V differences. This matches appendix 1-8 on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341.

1-9全书习题Book exercise已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第33页／PDF第42页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 33 / PDF p. 42. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

各元件功率：先统一节点，再判断关联方向Element power: solve nodes, then use passive signs

题目原文Transcribed question

电路如题图1-7所示，已知i₁=2 A，i₃=−3 A，u₁=10 V，u₄=−5 V，试求各元件吸收的功率。In Fig. 1-7, i₁ = 2 A, i₃ = −3 A, u₁ = 10 V and u₄ = −5 V. Find the power absorbed by each element.

四元件：1在上方，u₁左正右负而i₁向左；2在左竖支路，u₂上正下负；3在中竖支路，u₃上负下正而i₃向下；4在右竖支路，u₄上正下负。底部为共节点。Element 1 is on top, with u₁ positive left and i₁ leftward. Element 2 is on the left, u₂ positive at top. Element 3 is central, u₃ positive at bottom and i₃ downward. Element 4 is on the right, u₄ positive at top. The bottom is common.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

KCL；节点电位；关联方向p吸收=ui，非关联方向p吸收=−ui；总功率守恒。KCL, node potentials, absorbed p = ui for passive references or −ui for opposite references, and total power balance.
不能对所有元件机械使用ui。先由u₄和u₁求两个上节点电位，再用KCL补齐支路电流，逐个按“流入高参考端”的规则求吸收功率。Do not blindly use ui for every element. Determine the two upper potentials from u₄ and u₁, recover currents by KCL, then evaluate each absorbed power with its reference polarity.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

设底部为0 V求节点电位Set the bottom to zero and recover potentials

令右上节点B、左上节点A。u₄=V_B=−5 V，u₁=V_A−V_B=10 V，故V_A=5 V；于是u₂=5 V、u₃=0−V_B=5 V。Let B be upper right and A upper left. u₄ = V_B = −5 V and u₁ = V_A−V_B = 10 V, so V_A = 5 V. Therefore u₂ = 5 V and u₃ = 0−V_B = 5 V.

VB=−5,VA=5,u2=u3=5(V)

用KCL补齐元件2和4电流Use KCL for elements 2 and 4

元件2从A向下电流为i₂=i₁=2 A。定义元件4向下为i₄，B点流出代数和i₁+i₃+i₄=0，所以i₄=1 A。Element 2 carries i₂ = i₁ = 2 A downward from A. Define i₄ downward; the outgoing sum at B is i₁+i₃+i₄ = 0, giving i₄ = 1 A.

i2=2A,i4=−(2−3)=1A

逐个计算吸收功率Compute each absorbed power

1和3的图示电流均从电压负参考端流入，故用−ui；2和4用ui。元件3应为−5×(−3)=15 W。For 1 and 3 the drawn current enters the negative voltage-reference terminal, so use −ui. For 2 and 4 use ui. Element 3 absorbs −5×(−3) = 15 W.

p1=−u1i1=−20W,p2=u2i2=10W,p3=−u3i3=15W,p4=u4i4=−5W

用功率守恒审查答案Audit the answer with power balance

四元件吸收功率之和−20+10+15−5=0。1和4总共提供25 W，2和3总共吸收25 W。附录把元件3印成5 W会使总和变成−10 W，不满足守恒。The sum −20+10+15−5 = 0. Elements 1 and 4 deliver 25 W, while 2 and 3 absorb 25 W. The appendix value 5 W for element 3 instead gives −10 W total, violating conservation.

∑k=14pk=0

按元件1、2、3、4顺序：−20 W、10 W、15 W、−5 W。负值表示提供功率。附录第三个值5 W有误。For elements 1, 2, 3 and 4: −20 W, 10 W, 15 W and −5 W. Negative values mean delivered power. The appendix’s third value 5 W is wrong.

易错点Common errors

  • 元件3的u₃上负下正、i₃向下，属于非关联参考方向。For element 3 u₃ is positive at the bottom while i₃ points down, so the references are nonpassive.
  • 有符号的i₃=−3 A要直接代入，不能丢掉负号。Substitute the signed i₃ = −3 A without discarding its negative sign.

核验依据Verification

独立按物理支路首末节点计算p=(V起−V终)i，四项仍为−20、10、15、−5 W；全部节点KCL和总功率残差均为0。题干和完整极性原图第33页/PDF42页清楚；附录第332页/PDF341页印第三项5 W，与节点电位和功率守恒同时冲突。Independent physical branch calculation p = (V_start−V_end)i gives −20, 10, 15 and −5 W, with zero KCL residual at every node and zero power residual. The original statement and full polarity diagram on printed p. 33/PDF p. 42 are clear. The appendix’s third value 5 W on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341 conflicts with both node potentials and power conservation.

1-10全书习题Book exercise已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第33页／PDF第42页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 33 / PDF p. 42. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

对地电压求支路电流：分清4 Ω与4 SFrom node voltage to current: distinguish 4 Ω from 4 S

题目原文Transcribed question

电阻元件两端的对地电压如题图1-8所示，求通过各电阻元件的电流i。The terminal voltages relative to ground are shown in Fig. 1-8. Find current i through each resistive element.

(a)左端3 V、右端1 V，电阻4 Ω，i参考向左。(b)左端u₁、右端u₂，标注电导4 S，i参考向左。(a) Left terminal 3 V, right 1 V, resistance 4 Ω, with i leftward. (b) Left terminal u₁, right u₂, conductance 4 S, again with i leftward.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

欧姆定律i=ΔV/R=GΔV；S为西门子，1 S=1 Ω⁻¹；按电流箭头选电压差。Ohm’s law i = ΔV/R = GΔV, Siemens 1 S = 1 Ω⁻¹, and the voltage drop along the current reference.
两图电流都由右向左，因此电压差都取“右端减左端”。(a)给电阻，除以4；(b)给电导，乘以4。Both currents are referenced right to left, so use right-terminal minus left-terminal voltage. Divide by 4 for the resistance in (a), but multiply by 4 for the conductance in (b).
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

(a)电阻形式(a): resistance form

沿i向左的电压降为1−3=−2 V，除以4 Ω得到−0.5 A，实际电流向右。The voltage drop along leftward i is 1−3 = −2 V. Dividing by 4 Ω gives −0.5 A, actually rightward.

i=1−34=−0.5A

(b)电导形式(b): conductance form

图中4 S是电导，不是4 Ω。等效电阻为1/4 Ω；因此i=G(u₂−u₁)=4(u₂−u₁)。The original 4 S is conductance, not 4 Ω. Its equivalent resistance is 1/4 Ω, so i = G(u₂−u₁) = 4(u₂−u₁).

G=4S,R=14Ω,i=4(u2−u1)Awhen voltages are in V

(a) i=−0.5 A；(b) i=4(u₂−u₁) A（u₁、u₂以V计，原图给的是4 S电导）。(a) i = −0.5 A. (b) i = 4(u₂−u₁) A for voltages in volts; the original figure gives 4 S conductance.

易错点Common errors

  • S和Ω表示互为倒数的物理量，不能将(b)当成4 Ω。S and Ω describe reciprocal quantities; do not read (b) as 4 Ω.
  • 对地电位不能直接当电阻端电压，先沿参考箭头做两端差。A single ground-referenced potential is not the resistor voltage; take the terminal difference along the reference.

核验依据Verification

独立量纲与功率核验：(a)吸收功率(−2 V)(−0.5 A)=1 W；(b)S×V=A，且p=4(u₂−u₁)²≥0；以等效R=0.25 Ω计算得到相同电流。高分辨率原图确认4 S，附录第332页/PDF341页两问均正确。Independent dimensional and power checks: (a) absorbs (−2 V)(−0.5 A) = 1 W. (b) has S×V = A and p = 4(u₂−u₁)² ≥ 0; using equivalent R = 0.25 Ω gives the same current. High-resolution inspection confirms 4 S, and both appendix answers on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341 are correct.

1-11全书习题Book exercise已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第33页／PDF第42页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 33 / PDF p. 42. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

有分流的电阻链：从末端逐级累加电流A tapped resistor chain: accumulate current from the bottom

题目原文Transcribed question

电阻电路中各点对地电压及各支路电流如题图1-9所示，求各电阻的阻值。Ground-referenced node voltages and branch currents are shown in Fig. 1-9. Find all resistor values.

30 V经R₁至24 V节点，该处向右流出10 mA；R₂下接12 V节点，向右流出15 mA；R₃下接6 V节点，向右流出20 mA；R₄下接0 V，向下电流5 mA。30 V connects through R₁ to 24 V, which has 10 mA outgoing. R₂ leads to 12 V with 15 mA outgoing; R₃ to 6 V with 20 mA outgoing; R₄ to 0 V carries 5 mA downward.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

KCL累加分流；电阻端电压为相邻节点差；mA换算。KCL with outgoing taps, adjacent-node voltage differences and mA conversion.
这不是四只电阻同流的简单串联链。每层有分流，应从已知最底5 mA向上逐层加上流出的电流，再用各自压降除以各自电流。The four resistors do not share one current because taps draw current. Start from the given 5 mA at the bottom, add each outgoing tap upward, then divide each voltage drop by its own current.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

从R₄向上做KCLApply KCL upward from R₄

R₄电流为5 mA；R₃须另供20 mA，故25 mA；R₂须另供15 mA，故40 mA；R₁须另供10 mA，故50 mA。R₄ carries 5 mA. R₃ must also supply 20 mA, so it carries 25 mA; R₂ adds 15 mA to reach 40 mA; R₁ adds 10 mA to reach 50 mA.

I4=5,I3=25,I2=40,I1=50(mA)

用相邻节点压降求RUse adjacent-node drops for R

各段压降依次为6、12、6、6 V，除以各段电流50、40、25、5 mA。The respective voltage drops are 6, 12, 6 and 6 V. Divide by the corresponding currents 50, 40, 25 and 5 mA.

R1=60.05=120Ω,R2=120.04=300Ω,R3=60.025=240Ω,R4=60.005=1200Ω

R₁=120 Ω，R₂=300 Ω，R₃=240 Ω，R₄=1200 Ω。R₁ = 120 Ω, R₂ = 300 Ω, R₃ = 240 Ω and R₄ = 1200 Ω.

易错点Common errors

  • 每个节点有支路分流，不能令四只电阻电流相同。The node taps draw current, so the four resistor currents are unequal.
  • V/mA直接给出kΩ；若答案写Ω，先把mA化为A。V/mA gives kΩ; convert mA to A when reporting Ω.

核验依据Verification

独立逐节点回代KCL全部为0；源输入30×0.05=1.5 W，电阻共吸收0.96 W，三个外接端口带走24×0.01+12×0.015+6×0.02=0.54 W，和为1.5 W。高分辨率附录第332页/PDF341页R₄实际印为1200 Ω，与结果一致。Independent node-by-node substitution gives zero KCL residual. Input power is 30×0.05 = 1.5 W; resistors absorb 0.96 W and the three external ports take 24×0.01+12×0.015+6×0.02 = 0.54 W, totaling 1.5 W. High-resolution appendix inspection confirms R₄ = 1200 Ω on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341, matching the result.

1-12全书习题Book exercise已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第33页／PDF第42页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 33 / PDF p. 42. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

理想电压源并联电阻：电压固定，源电流调整A resistor across an ideal voltage source: voltage fixed, source current adjusts

题目原文Transcribed question

电路如题图1-10所示，其中Uₛ为理想电压源，试判断当外电路不变，仅电阻R变化时，将会引起电路中哪条支路电流的变化。In Fig. 1-10, Uₛ is an ideal voltage source. With the external circuit unchanged, determine which branch current changes when only R changes.

理想电压源Uₛ上正下负，与R并联，共同接到右侧外电路端口；端口u上正下负，外电流i由上端向右流出。An ideal top-positive voltage source Uₛ is parallel with R and the right external port. Port u is positive at the top and external current i leaves the upper terminal to the right.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

理想电压源固定端电压；并联电压相同；KCL；固定外电路的工作点。Fixed ideal-source voltage, common parallel voltage, KCL and the operating point of an unchanged external circuit.
Uₛ直接钳定外端口电压u=Uₛ。R变化会改变自身电流，但外电路输入电压未变，因此其原工作电流i不变；理想源会补足R电流变化。Uₛ directly fixes port voltage u = Uₛ. Changing R changes its own current. The external circuit retains the same voltage and its operating current i; the ideal source supplies the extra resistor current.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

先固定端口电压Fix the port voltage first

理想电压源与外端口并联，故u始终等于Uₛ。对工作点确定的固定外电路，i由这个固定端电压决定，因此不随R变化。The ideal voltage source is across the external port, so u remains Uₛ. For an unchanged external circuit with a determined operating point, i is fixed by this voltage and does not change with R.

u=Us,iexternal unchanged

写出R支路及电源支路电流Write resistor and source currents

定义R中向下电流i_R，电源从下向上供出电流i_s。则i_R=Uₛ/R，节点KCL为i_s=i+i_R。一般R变化时，i_R和i_s都相应变化；端口i不变。Define i_R downward through R and i_s upward supplied by the source. Then i_R = Uₛ/R and KCL gives i_s = i+i_R. In general both i_R and i_s change as R changes, while external i remains fixed.

iR=UsR,is=i+UsR

补充附录简答省略的源电流Complete the appendix’s abbreviated statement

附录只写“电阻R支路的电流变化”，说明了R支路的变化，但若把电源支路也计入题目，电源电流必须同步调整，不能宣称其恒定。若Uₛ=0等退化情况，具体变化由上述公式判断。The appendix states only that the resistor-branch current changes. This identifies R’s response, but if the source branch is included, its current must adjust as well. The formulas also cover degenerate cases such as Uₛ = 0.

一般R变化会使R支路电流i_R=Uₛ/R变化，理想电压源供出电流i_s=i+i_R也随之调整；外电路电流i不变。In general changing R changes i_R = Uₛ/R and also the ideal source’s supplied current i_s = i+i_R. External current i is unchanged.

易错点Common errors

  • 理想电压源固定的是电压，不是自身电流。An ideal voltage source fixes its voltage, not its current.
  • “外电路电流不变”不能推广成“源支路电流也不变”。Unchanged external current does not imply unchanged source current.

核验依据Verification

独立数值反例与功率核验：Uₛ=10 V，固定外负载10 Ω。R从10 Ω变到5 Ω时，外电流保持1 A；R电流1→2 A，源电流2→3 A。源功率20→30 W，恰等于外负载10 W加R功率10→20 W。附录第332页/PDF341页定性简答省略了这项源电流调整，记录中已补全。Independent numerical and power check: with Uₛ = 10 V and fixed external 10 Ω load, changing R from 10 to 5 Ω leaves external current at 1 A, increases resistor current from 1 to 2 A and source current from 2 to 3 A. Source power rises from 20 to 30 W, balancing the fixed 10 W external load plus 10 to 20 W in R. The qualitative appendix answer on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341 omits the source-current adjustment, which is included here.

1-13全书习题Book exercise已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第33页／PDF第42页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 33 / PDF p. 42. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

含源支路特性：下降斜率的绝对值就是RA source-containing branch: resistance is the negative slope

题目原文Transcribed question

一段含源支路及其u-i特性如题图1-11所示，图中3条直线对应于电阻R的3个不同数值R₁、R₂、R₃，试根据该图判断各电阻的大小关系。A source-containing branch and its u-i characteristics are shown in Fig. 1-11. The three lines correspond to R₁, R₂ and R₃. Determine their ordering.

串联电压源Uₛ上正下负、上方电阻R，电流i向上并从正端口流出；端电压u上正下负。u-i图纵轴u、横轴i，R₁线水平，R₂缓降，R₃陡降，共同纵截距Uₛ。A top-positive source Uₛ is series with R above it. Current i flows upward and leaves the positive port; u is top-positive. The plot has u vertical and i horizontal: R₁ horizontal, R₂ gently descending, R₃ steeply descending, all intercepting Uₛ.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

KVL；含源支路端口特性；u-i图斜率du/di=−R。KVL, source-containing port characteristics and slope du/di = −R.
先按端口电流“流出正端”的参考方向写u=Uₛ−Ri。电阻越大，曲线随电流下降越快；不要套用无源电阻的正斜率。Because current leaves the positive port, the branch law is u = Uₛ−Ri. Greater resistance gives a steeper downward line; do not use the positive slope of a passive resistor reference.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

从原图写端口关系Write the port law from the figure

电流从源正端向上流过R，电阻压降为Ri，端口剩余电压为Uₛ−Ri。Current rises from the source’s positive terminal through R, dropping Ri. The remaining port voltage is Uₛ−Ri.

u=Us−Ri,dudi=−R

用斜率绝对值比较RCompare negative-slope magnitudes

R₁曲线水平，所以R₁=0；R₃比R₂下降更陡，因此R₃>R₂>0。R₁’s line is horizontal, giving R₁ = 0. R₃ descends more steeply than R₂, so R₃ > R₂ > 0.

R1=0<R2<R3

R₁=0，且R₁<R₂<R₃。R₁ = 0 and R₁ < R₂ < R₃.

易错点Common errors

  • 图的横轴为i、纵轴为u，斜率单位才是Ω；轴反过来则读的是电导。With i horizontal and u vertical, the slope has units Ω; reversed axes would describe conductance.
  • 此题电流流出正端口，特性斜率为−R，不能直接用+R。Here current leaves the positive port, so the slope is −R rather than +R.

核验依据Verification

独立固定正电流比较：三条线同截距Uₛ，若i>0，则u₁>u₂>u₃；由R=(Uₛ−u)/i即得R₁<R₂<R₃。再以有限差分检查Δu/Δi=−R。与附录第332页/PDF341页1-13一致。Independent fixed-current comparison: all lines share intercept Uₛ; for i > 0, u₁ > u₂ > u₃. Then R = (Uₛ−u)/i gives R₁ < R₂ < R₃. A finite-difference check also gives Δu/Δi = −R. This matches appendix 1-13 on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341.

1-14全书习题Book exercise已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第33页／PDF第42页。范围状态：讲授范围内。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 33 / PDF p. 42. Coverage: within the taught chapter.

电流源网络：外节点KCL直接确定电阻压降Current-source network: outer-node KCL fixes resistor drops

题目原文Transcribed question

求题图1-12所示电路中的u_ae、u_be、u_cf、u_df、u_ef。Find u_ae, u_be, u_cf, u_df and u_ef in Fig. 1-12.

外矩形节点a左下、b左上、c右上、d右下，内部e左、f右。电流源a→b为1 A，b→c为2 A，a→d为3 A，d→c为4 A。电阻b-e为5 Ω、a-e为4 Ω、c-f为10 Ω、d-f为8 Ω、e-f为20 Ω。Outer nodes are a lower left, b upper left, c upper right, d lower right; inner e left and f right. Sources: a→b 1 A, b→c 2 A, a→d 3 A, d→c 4 A. Resistors: b-e 5 Ω, a-e 4 Ω, c-f 10 Ω, d-f 8 Ω, e-f 20 Ω.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

KCL；电阻电压u_xy=V_x−V_y；电流源方向；节点法与全电路功率守恒。KCL, u_xy = V_x−V_y, source directions, nodal analysis and full-network power conservation.
四个外节点各只连一只电阻，另两支路是已知电流源，因此每只外围电阻的电流可以直接由KCL确定。再到e点求20 Ω电流；无需先解大量未知电压。Each outer node has one resistor and two known current sources, so KCL directly determines each outer resistor current. Then use node e for the 20 Ω current, avoiding an unnecessary large voltage calculation.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

左侧a、b节点Use left nodes a and b

a向外流出1+3=4 A，故e→a为4 A，u_ae=−4×4=−16 V。b流入1 A、流出2 A，故e→b为1 A，u_be=−1×5=−5 V。At a, sources take 1+3 = 4 A outward, so e→a carries 4 A and u_ae = −4×4 = −16 V. At b, 1 A enters and 2 A leaves, so e→b is 1 A and u_be = −1×5 = −5 V.

uae=−16V,ube=−5V

右侧c、d节点Use right nodes c and d

c收到2+4=6 A，故c→f为6 A，u_cf=6×10=60 V。d收到3 A、流出4 A，故f→d为1 A，u_df=−1×8=−8 V。At c, sources deliver 2+4 = 6 A, so c→f carries 6 A and u_cf = 6×10 = 60 V. At d, 3 A enters and 4 A leaves, so f→d is 1 A and u_df = −1×8 = −8 V.

ucf=60V,udf=−8V

e节点求中央20 Ω压降Use node e for the central 20 Ω drop

e向a、b分别流出4 A、1 A，总共5 A，所以中央支路必须由f向e流入5 A。u_ef=−5×20=−100 V。e sends 4 A to a and 1 A to b, so 5 A must enter from f. Therefore u_ef = −5×20 = −100 V.

ie→f=−5A,uef=−100V

建立一组节点电位用于回代Construct node potentials for substitution

取V_e=0，可得V_a=−16、V_b=−5、V_f=100、V_c=160、V_d=92 V。它们重现五个所求电压以及每个电流源节点的KCL。With V_e = 0, obtain V_a = −16, V_b = −5, V_f = 100, V_c = 160 and V_d = 92 V. These reproduce all five requested differences and source-node KCL.

(Va,Vb,Vc,Vd,Ve,Vf)=(−16,−5,160,92,0,100)V

u_ae=−16 V；u_be=−5 V；u_cf=60 V；u_df=−8 V；u_ef=−100 V。u_ae = −16 V; u_be = −5 V; u_cf = 60 V; u_df = −8 V; u_ef = −100 V.

易错点Common errors

  • u_ae表示V_a−V_e；不能只把电阻压降大小当作答案。u_ae means V_a−V_e; the unsigned drop magnitude is insufficient.
  • d点电流源3 A流入、4 A流出，因此电阻实际电流必须由f流向d。At d the sources provide 3 A inward but 4 A outward, so the resistor current must run from f to d.

核验依据Verification

独立按原图搭建五节点电导矩阵，以有理数高斯消元求得V_a=−16、V_b=−5、V_c=160、V_d=92、V_f=100 V（V_e=0），全部节点残差精确为0。五电阻功率为5、64、360、8、500 W，总937 W；四源吸收功率−11、−330、−324、−272 W，总−937 W，全电路功率和0。与附录第332页/PDF341页1-14五项全部一致。An independent five-node conductance matrix stamped from the original topology and solved by exact rational Gaussian elimination gives V_a = −16, V_b = −5, V_c = 160, V_d = 92 and V_f = 100 V with V_e = 0; every nodal residual is exactly zero. Resistor powers 5, 64, 360, 8 and 500 W total 937 W. Source absorbed powers −11, −330, −324 and −272 W total −937 W, giving zero overall power. All five values match appendix 1-14 on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341.

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第34页／PDF第43页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 34 / PDF p. 43. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：1-24Assigned requirement: 1-24

先由电流求电阻压降，再求电流源端电压Find current-source voltages from resistor drops

题目原文Transcribed question

电路如题图1-22所示，求两个电流源两端的电压U₁、U₂。For the circuit shown in Fig. 1-22, find the voltages U₁ and U₂ across the two current sources.

左支路为3 A向下电流源，U₁上正下负。中支路为2 Ω电阻串联5 V电压源，电压源上正下负。上节点经过1 Ω接右支路2 A向下电流源，U₂上正下负，其下串联3 Ω回到底节点。Left branch: 3 A current source downward, U₁ positive at the top. Middle branch: 2 Ω resistor in series with a 5 V source positive at the top. The upper node connects through 1 Ω to a downward 2 A current source with U₂ positive at the top, followed by 3 Ω to the lower node.

本题图在书第35页／PDF第44页。The source figure is on printed p. 35 / PDF p. 44.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

KCL、KVL、节点电位与电压参考极性。KCL, KVL, node potentials, and voltage reference polarity.
电流源已经确定其支路电流。先对上下节点用KCL，再沿已知电压源的支路求节点电位，最后求两个电流源电压。The current sources fix their branch currents. Use KCL at the common nodes, find node potentials through the branch containing the known voltage source, then compute the source voltages.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

先确定中支路电流Find the middle-branch current

取底节点为0 V。3 A和2 A都向下流出顶节点，因此中支路向上电流为5 A，即按向下参考方向为-5 A。Set the lower node to 0 V. The 3 A and 2 A branches both leave the upper node downward, so the middle branch carries 5 A upward, or -5 A under a downward reference.

im+3+2=0⇒im=−5A

求U₁Find U₁

5 V电压源上端电位为5 V；2 Ω电阻按自上而下的压降为2×(-5)=-10 V，所以顶节点电位为-5 V。U₁按上正下负，等于-5 V。The upper terminal of the 5 V source is at 5 V. The downward voltage drop across 2 Ω is 2(-5) = -10 V, making the upper node -5 V. Since U₁ is referenced positive at the top, U₁ = -5 V.

U1=5+2(−5)=−5V

求U₂Find U₂

右支路电流为2 A。1 Ω电阻压降2 V，故电流源上端电位为-7 V；3 Ω电阻上端电位为6 V。按U₂的上正下负参考极性相减。The right branch carries 2 A. The 1 Ω resistor drops 2 V, so the source upper terminal is at -7 V. The upper terminal of 3 Ω is at 6 V. Subtract according to U₂’s top-positive polarity.

U2=(−5−2×1)−(2×3)=−13V

U₁=-5 V，U₂=-13 V；两者实际电压极性均与图示参考极性相反。U₁ = -5 V and U₂ = -13 V. Both actual voltage polarities oppose the indicated reference polarities.

易错点Common errors

  • 电流源并不自动规定其端电压为正；电流箭头和电压极性是不同的参考信息。A current source does not force its terminal voltage to be positive; the current arrow and voltage polarity are separate references.
  • 题干在书第34页，图在第35页，必须同时查看。The statement is on printed p. 34 and the figure on p. 35; both pages are required.

核验依据Verification

右支路KVL：2+(-13)+6=-5 V，与U₁一致。三电阻耗散50+4+12=66 W；3 A源、2 A源和5 V源吸收功率分别为-15、-26、-25 W，合计-66 W，功率守恒。书第332页（PDF341页）给出相同的-5 V、-13 V。Right-branch KVL gives 2 + (-13) + 6 = -5 V, matching U₁. The resistors dissipate 50 + 4 + 12 = 66 W; the 3 A, 2 A, and 5 V sources absorb -15, -26, and -25 W, totaling -66 W. Power balances. Printed p. 332 (PDF p. 341) gives the same -5 V and -13 V.

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第35页／PDF第44页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 35 / PDF p. 44. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：1-26Assigned requirement: 1-26

受控电压源：把控制关系与KVL一起列出Controlled voltage source: combine its law with KVL

题目原文Transcribed question

电路题图1-24所示，求在下列条件下受控源的电压：(1) uₛ=2 V；(2) uₛ=8 V。For the circuit in Fig. 1-24, find the controlled-source voltage for (1) uₛ = 2 V and (2) uₛ = 8 V.

左侧独立电压源uₛ上正下负；上方2 Ω电阻电压u₁左正右负；右侧受控电压源3u₁上正下负，三者组成单回路。The independent voltage source uₛ on the left is positive at the top. The upper 2 Ω resistor has u₁ positive on the left. The right controlled voltage source is 3u₁, positive at the top. All three form one loop.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

KVL；电压控制电压源的控制关系。KVL and the voltage-controlled voltage-source relation.
受控源的电压不是额外给定的常数。将3u₁作为同一未知量的倍数，整圈只剩一个电压未知量。The controlled-source voltage is not an independent constant. Writing it as 3u₁ leaves only one unknown voltage in the loop.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

列回路电压关系Write the loop voltage relation

从左下沿顺时针绕行：独立源提供电压上升uₛ，随后两段压降为u₁与3u₁。Going clockwise from the lower left, the independent source gives a rise uₛ, followed by drops u₁ and 3u₁.

us−u1−3u1=0

先得到通用式Obtain a general expression

因此u₁=uₛ/4；题目要求的是受控源电压3u₁，而不是电阻电压u₁。Thus u₁ = uₛ/4. The requested voltage is 3u₁ across the controlled source, not u₁ across the resistor.

ud=3u1=34us

代入两个条件Substitute both conditions

uₛ=2 V时，u₁=0.5 V，受控源电压1.5 V；uₛ=8 V时，u₁=2 V，受控源电压6 V。For uₛ = 2 V, u₁ = 0.5 V and the controlled-source voltage is 1.5 V. For uₛ = 8 V, u₁ = 2 V and the controlled-source voltage is 6 V.

ud(1)=1.5V,ud(2)=6V

(1) 1.5 V；(2) 6 V，均按图示上正下负参考极性。(1) 1.5 V; (2) 6 V, with the indicated top-positive polarity.

易错点Common errors

  • 不要把受控源当成独立源另设任意数值，也不要把u₁作为最终答案。Do not treat the dependent source as an arbitrary independent value or report u₁ as the requested answer.

核验依据Verification

分别回代KVL：0.5+1.5=2 V，2+6=8 V；同时满足受控关系u_d=3u₁。书第332页/PDF341页1-26给出相同的1.5 V、6 V。Substitution gives 0.5 + 1.5 = 2 V and 2 + 6 = 8 V, and both satisfy u_d = 3u₁. Answer 1-26 on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341 gives the same 1.5 V and 6 V.

指定要求：1-30Assigned requirement: 1-30

受控电流源功率：先电流，后端电压Controlled current-source power: find current and voltage

题目原文Transcribed question

题图1-28所示电路中各参数已给定，试求受控电流源的功率，并说明是吸收功率还是提供功率。All parameters of the circuit in Fig. 1-28 are given. Find the power of the controlled current source and state whether it absorbs or delivers power.

底部为公共节点。左节点经1 Ω接底部；左、中节点间2 Ω，中、右节点间4 Ω，左、右节点间上支路3 Ω。中节点经上正下负的6 V电压源接底部，I₁参考向上。右节点向下先经过上负下正的12 V源，再经过大小I₁/3、方向向下的受控电流源到底部。The bottom is common. The left node connects to it through 1 Ω. There are 2 Ω between left and middle, 4 Ω between middle and right, and 3 Ω between left and right. A 6 V source, positive at the middle node, connects middle to bottom, with I₁ upward. From the right node downward, a 12 V source is negative at the top and positive at the bottom, followed by a downward controlled current source I₁/3.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

KCL、KVL、受控电流源；关联参考方向下的功率。KCL, KVL, controlled current sources, and passive-sign power.
6 V源先给出中节点电位。受控源电流由I₁决定，端电压则须把右节点电位与12 V源极性一起计算，不能只用I₁/3求功率。The 6 V source fixes the middle-node potential. The dependent-source current follows from I₁, but its voltage also depends on the right-node potential and 12 V source polarity.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

定义电位和控制电流Define node potentials and control current

取底节点为0 V，左、右节点分别为vL、vR，中节点为6 V。中节点KCL把I₁写成两个电阻电流之和。以下电压用V、电阻用Ω。Set the bottom to 0 V, the left and right potentials to vL and vR, and the middle potential to 6 V. Middle-node KCL expresses I₁ as the sum of two resistor currents. Voltages are in V and resistances in Ω.

I1=6−vL2+6−vR4

对左右节点列KCLApply KCL at the left and right nodes

左节点的三条电阻支路电流相加为0；右节点向下支路电流由受控源固定为I₁/3。Sum the three resistor currents at the left node. At the right node, the downward branch current is fixed to I₁/3 by the controlled source.

vL1+vL−62+vL−vR3=0,vR−vL3+vR−64+I13=0

消元求电流Eliminate variables and find current

将I₁代入右节点方程，化为(vR−vL)/2=0，故vR=vL。左节点方程随后给出vL=vR=2 V。于是I₁=2+1=3 A，受控源电流为1 A向下。Substituting I₁ in the right-node equation reduces it to (vR−vL)/2 = 0. Hence vR = vL; the left-node equation gives both as 2 V. Then I₁ = 2 + 1 = 3 A and the controlled source carries 1 A downward.

vL=vR=2V,I1=3A,Id=1A

根据12 V源极性求端电压和功率Use the 12 V polarity to find voltage and power

12 V源下正上负，因此受控电流源上端电位为vR+12=14 V。自行取其端电压上正下负，与向下电流构成关联方向，故吸收功率为正。The 12 V source is positive at its lower terminal, placing the controlled-source upper terminal at vR + 12 = 14 V. Referencing its voltage positive at the top is passive with the downward current, so its absorbed power is positive.

ud=2+12=14V,pd,abs=udId=14W

受控电流源吸收14 W。The controlled current source absorbs 14 W.

易错点Common errors

  • 12 V源的上端标“−”、下端标“+”，不能把受控源上端电位误写成vR−12。The 12 V source is negative at its upper terminal and positive at its lower terminal; the controlled-source upper potential is vR + 12.
  • 受控源是电源模型，但仍可能吸收功率；必须依据端电压与电流的共同参考方向判断。A controlled source can absorb power. Determine the sign from both its voltage and current references.

核验依据Verification

独立功率平衡：1、2、4 Ω电阻分别耗散4、8、4 W，3 Ω电流为0；6 V源吸收−18 W，12 V源吸收−12 W，受控源吸收14 W，总和16−18−12+14=0。书第332页/PDF341页1-30也给出14 W、吸收功率。Independent power balance: the 1, 2 and 4 Ω resistors dissipate 4, 8 and 4 W; the 3 Ω current is zero. The 6 V and 12 V sources absorb −18 and −12 W, and the controlled source absorbs 14 W: 16−18−12+14 = 0. Answer 1-30 on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341 is also 14 W absorbed.

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第35页／PDF第44页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 35 / PDF p. 44. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：1-32Assigned requirement: 1-32

交叉线不相连：开关前后的等效电阻Unconnected crossing wires: resistance before and after switching

题目原文Transcribed question

求题图1-30所示单口网络分别在开关S打开和闭合时的等效电阻。Find the equivalent resistance of the one-port network in Fig. 1-30 with switch S open and with S closed.

a在左上，b在左下，右上与右下两节点由开关S连接。a到右上为36 Ω，a到右下斜支路也为36 Ω；b到右下为24 Ω，b到右上斜支路也为24 Ω。两条斜线在中央交叉处无连接点，互不连接。Terminal a is upper left and b lower left. Switch S connects the upper-right and lower-right nodes. There are 36 Ω from a to each right node and 24 Ω from b to each right node. The crossing diagonal wires have no junction and are not connected.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

按节点判断串并联；无连接点的交叉线。Series/parallel reduction by nodes and unconnected wire crossings.
先按导线连接关系重画，不能把中央交叉当节点。开关打开时是两条独立串联支路，闭合时是两组并联电阻串联。Redraw by connectivity, keeping the central crossing disconnected. With S open there are two series paths in parallel; with S closed, two parallel groups are in series.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

开关打开Switch open

a经任一右侧节点到b都必须依次经过36 Ω和24 Ω，每条路径60 Ω；两条路径只在a、b端相接。Each a-to-b path through a right node contains 36 Ω and 24 Ω in series, making 60 Ω. The two paths meet only at a and b.

Ropen=(36+24)∥(36+24)=30Ω

开关闭合Switch closed

两个右侧节点被短接合并。a到该公共节点有两个36 Ω并联，b到该节点有两个24 Ω并联，再将两组串联。The two right nodes merge. The two 36 Ω resistors from a are parallel, as are the two 24 Ω resistors from b. These groups are then in series.

Rclosed=(36∥36)+(24∥24)=18+12=30Ω

解释为何数值相同Explain the unchanged value

原来两条路径具有相同分压比例，所以右侧两个节点本来等电位。连接等电位节点不会改变端口电流。The two original paths have identical divider ratios, so the right nodes are already at equal potentials. Connecting them does not change terminal current.

S打开：30 Ω；S闭合：30 Ω。S open: 30 Ω. S closed: 30 Ω.

易错点Common errors

  • 中央导线交叉无黑点，不可自行增加连接。开关闭合也不等于把整个端口短路。Do not add a connection at the unmarked central crossing. Closing S does not short the entire port.

核验依据Verification

独立1 V测试：令Va=1 V、Vb=0，开关打开时两个右侧节点均为24/(36+24)=0.4 V；总测试电流为2×(1−0.4)/36=1/30 A，故Req=30 Ω。等电位使闭合时开关电流为0，结果仍30 Ω。书第332页/PDF341页1-32为30 Ω、30 Ω。Independent 1 V test: with Va = 1 V and Vb = 0, both right nodes are at 24/(36+24) = 0.4 V. Total test current is 2(1−0.4)/36 = 1/30 A, giving 30 Ω. The closed switch carries zero current between equal potentials, preserving 30 Ω. Answer 1-32 on printed p. 332/PDF p. 341 gives 30 Ω in both cases.

指定要求：1-45Assigned requirement: 1-45

电源等效变换：先合并为诺顿源Source transformation: combine Norton sources first

题目原文Transcribed question

利用电源等效变换求题图1-43所示电路中的电流i。Use source equivalence transformations to find the current i in Fig. 1-43.

三个支路并联于上、下公共节点。左支路为1 Ω电阻串联9 V电压源（上正下负）；中支路为2 Ω串联4 V电压源（上负下正）；右支路7 Ω，i向下。Three branches share upper and lower nodes. The left has 1 Ω in series with a 9 V source positive at the top. The middle has 2 Ω in series with a 4 V source positive at the bottom. The right has 7 Ω with i downward.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

电压源与串联电阻的诺顿等效；电流源并联；分流公式。Norton transformation of a voltage source and series resistor; parallel sources; current division.
两电压源的正端相反，变换后电流源方向也相反。先作带符号的电流源合并，再对7 Ω负载分流。The sources have opposite polarities, so their transformed current sources oppose. Combine their signed currents before finding the 7 Ω branch current.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

变换左支路Transform the left branch

9 V串联1 Ω等效为9 A向上电流源并联1 Ω，使上节点开路电位为正。The 9 V source with 1 Ω becomes a 9 A upward current source in parallel with 1 Ω, preserving a positive open-circuit upper potential.

IN1=9/1=9A(up)

变换中支路并合并Transform and combine the middle branch

4 V源正端在底部，等效电流源为2 A向下。两源净电流为7 A向上，内阻为1 Ω与2 Ω并联。The 4 V source is positive at the bottom, so its Norton current is 2 A downward. The net current is 7 A upward, with 1 Ω and 2 Ω in parallel.

IN=9−4/2=7A,RN=1∥2=23Ω

计算负载分流Find load current by division

7 A在内阻2/3 Ω和负载7 Ω之间分流。i沿7 Ω向下，为正。The 7 A divides between 2/3 Ω and the 7 Ω load. Current i is positive downward through the load.

i=INRNRN+7=72/32/3+7=1423A≈0.608696A

i=14/23 A≈0.609 A，向下。i = 14/23 A ≈ 0.609 A, downward.

易错点Common errors

  • 中支路4 V源的正端在下方，不能把两个诺顿电流源都取向上。The 4 V source is positive at the lower terminal; its Norton source must oppose the 9 A source.
  • 源等效保持端口特性，不保证变换前后内部元件的功率逐项相同。Source equivalence preserves port behavior, not every internal element power.

核验依据Verification

直接对原电路独立列KCL：(V−9)/1+(V+4)/2+V/7=0，得V=98/23 V，i=V/7=14/23 A。书第333页/PDF342页1-45列0.61 A，与精确值四舍五入一致。Independent original-circuit KCL gives (V−9)/1 + (V+4)/2 + V/7 = 0, so V = 98/23 V and i = V/7 = 14/23 A. Answer 1-45 on printed p. 333/PDF p. 342 gives 0.61 A, matching the rounded exact value.

2-3指定作业Assigned已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第61页／PDF第70页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 61 / PDF p. 70. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：2-3Assigned requirement: 2-3

一棵树确定全部基本割集与基本回路One spanning tree determines all fundamental cutsets and loops

题目原文Transcribed question

在题图2-1(b)中选树T(3,4,5,6)，分别找出对应的基本割集和基本回路。In Fig. 2-1(b), choose the spanning tree T = (3, 4, 5, 6). Find the corresponding fundamental cutsets and fundamental loops.

为说明拓扑，临时将左、中、右上、左下、右下节点依次命名A、B、C、D、E。有向支路为1:A→D，2:A→B，3:C→A，4:D→B，5:B→C，6:B→E，7:E→C，8:E→D。图中的G为图名。For description, label the left, center, upper-right, lower-left and lower-right nodes A, B, C, D and E. Directed edges are 1: A→D, 2: A→B, 3: C→A, 4: D→B, 5: B→C, 6: B→E, 7: E→C and 8: E→D. G in the source figure denotes the graph.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

树、树支、连支、基本割集与基本回路定义。Spanning trees, twigs, links, fundamental cutsets and fundamental loops.
基本割集逐根删除树支求；基本回路逐根加入连支求。每组必须恰含一个对应树支或连支，不能枚举任意割集、任意回路。Find each fundamental cutset by deleting one twig, and each fundamental loop by adding one link. Use the specified tree rather than enumerating arbitrary cuts or loops.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

确定树支和连支数量Identify twigs and links

全图5个节点、8条支路。给定4条树支3、4、5、6连通全图且不成环；剩余连支为1、2、7、8。The graph has 5 nodes and 8 edges. Twigs 3, 4, 5 and 6 connect all nodes without a cycle. Links are 1, 2, 7 and 8.

n=5, b=8,n−1=4,b−n+1=4

删树支找基本割集Delete each twig for its fundamental cutset

删3后A与其余节点分开，跨分区边为1、2、3；删4后D孤立，跨边为1、4、8；删5后{A,C}与{B,D,E}分开，跨边为1、2、5、7；删6后E孤立，跨边为6、7、8。Deleting 3 isolates A, giving edges 1,2,3. Deleting 4 isolates D, giving 1,4,8. Deleting 5 separates {A,C} from {B,D,E}, giving 1,2,5,7. Deleting 6 isolates E, giving 6,7,8.

C3={1,2,3},C4={1,4,8},C5={1,2,5,7},C6={6,7,8}

加连支找基本回路Add each link for its fundamental loop

加入连支1，沿树中D→B→C→A闭合，得到{1,3,4,5}；加入2得到{2,3,5}；加入7得到{5,6,7}；加入8得到{4,6,8}。集合内部顺序不影响答案。Adding link 1 closes through the tree path D→B→C→A, giving {1,3,4,5}. Adding links 2, 7 and 8 gives {2,3,5}, {5,6,7} and {4,6,8}. Set ordering does not matter.

L1={1,3,4,5},L2={2,3,5},L7={5,6,7},L8={4,6,8}

基本割集：{1,2,3}、{1,4,8}、{1,2,5,7}、{6,7,8}；基本回路：{1,3,4,5}、{2,3,5}、{5,6,7}、{4,6,8}。Fundamental cutsets: {1,2,3}, {1,4,8}, {1,2,5,7}, {6,7,8}. Fundamental loops: {1,3,4,5}, {2,3,5}, {5,6,7}, {4,6,8}.

易错点Common errors

  • 基本割集恰含一条树支，其余为连支；基本回路恰含一条连支，其余为树支。A fundamental cutset contains exactly one twig; a fundamental loop contains exactly one link.
  • 题目只问支路集合；若进一步列KCL/KVL，应另按选定割集或回路方向处理正负号。This question asks for edge sets. Signed KCL/KVL equations additionally require cutset or loop orientations.

核验依据Verification

用保存的有向边表独立进行删边连通分量与树路径检查：4个割集均恰含相应一个树支，4个回路均恰含相应一个连支，数量符合n−1=4和b−n+1=4。书第333页/PDF342页2-3的所有集合相同（书中回路元素顺序略有不同）。An independent edge-table check of components after twig deletion and paths in the tree confirms every cutset and loop; each has exactly its one designated twig or link. Counts match n−1 = 4 and b−n+1 = 4. All sets match answer 2-3 on printed p. 333/PDF p. 342, regardless of listed element order.

指定要求：2-11Assigned requirement: 2-11

节点法：先列注入电流，再求端口电压差Nodal analysis: injections first, port difference last

题目原文Transcribed question

试用节点电压法求题图2-9所示电路中的电压u。Use nodal-voltage analysis to find voltage u in Fig. 2-9.

底左公共节点接地，15 V源上正下负，其上端经3 Ω接节点1。节点1经6 Ω接地，经2 Ω接节点2；另有5 A源从节点1指向节点2。节点2经2 Ω接节点3，节点3经2 Ω接地。输出u按节点2正、节点3负。The lower-left common node is ground. A 15 V source positive at the top connects through 3 Ω to node 1. Node 1 connects to ground through 6 Ω and to node 2 through 2 Ω, with a 5 A source also pointing from 1 to 2. Node 2 connects through 2 Ω to node 3, which connects through 2 Ω to ground. Output u is positive at node 2 and negative at node 3.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

节点电压法；电流源的注入符号；电压等于节点电位差。Nodal analysis, current-source injection signs and voltage as a node-potential difference.
5 A在节点1流出、节点2流入，两条方程右端符号相反。输出端负极是节点3，不能直接把节点2电位当输出。The 5 A source leaves node 1 and enters node 2, so its injection signs oppose. The output negative terminal is node 3, not ground.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

节点1列KCLKCL at node 1

取节点电压为v₁、v₂、v₃，源上端已知15 V。节点1电阻流出电流之和等于−5 A。Let node potentials be v₁, v₂ and v₃. The source upper terminal is at 15 V. Resistor currents leaving node 1 sum to −5 A.

v1−153+v16+v1−v22=−5

节点2、3列KCLKCL at nodes 2 and 3

节点2注入5 A，节点3没有外部注入。Node 2 receives 5 A; node 3 has no external injection.

v2−v12+v2−v32=5,v3−v22+v32=0

消元求各节点电位Solve node potentials

第一式化为v₂=2v₁，第三式给v₂=2v₃；代入第二式，得到v₁=v₃=5 V、v₂=10 V。The first equation gives v₂ = 2v₁ and the third gives v₂ = 2v₃. Substitution into the second gives v₁ = v₃ = 5 V and v₂ = 10 V.

v1=5V,v2=10V,v3=5V

按输出参考极性取电位差Take the referenced output difference

输出u的正端是2，负端是3。The positive output terminal is 2 and the negative terminal is 3.

u=v2−v3=10−5=5V

u=5 V。u = 5 V.

易错点Common errors

  • 同一个5 A源在两个节点的注入符号相反，不能两处都写+5。The same 5 A source has opposite injection signs at nodes 1 and 2.
  • 节点3不是地，u=v₂−v₃，而非v₂。Node 3 is not ground: u = v₂−v₃, not v₂.

核验依据Verification

独立源变换复核：节点1左侧15 V/3 Ω与6 Ω网络等效为10 V串联2 Ω；节点1到2的5 A∥2 Ω变为使节点2升高10 V的串联源。外回路总电阻2+2+4=8 Ω，电流(10+10)/8=2.5 A，故节点2为10 V、节点3为5 V，输出5 V。书第333页/PDF342页2-11亦为5 V。Independent source-transformation check: the left 15 V/3 Ω and 6 Ω network becomes 10 V in series with 2 Ω. The 5 A parallel 2 Ω between nodes 1 and 2 becomes a 10 V rise toward node 2 in series with 2 Ω. The loop current is (10+10)/(2+2+4) = 2.5 A, giving node 2 at 10 V and node 3 at 5 V, hence u = 5 V. Answer 2-11 on printed p. 333/PDF p. 342 agrees.

2-12指定作业Assigned已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第62页／PDF第71页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 62 / PDF p. 71. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：2-12Assigned requirement: 2-12

受控电压源：先读正负端，再写约束Controlled voltage source: polarity before constraints

题目原文Transcribed question

求题图2-10所示电路中的电流i。Find current i in the circuit of Fig. 2-10.

上下为公共节点。左支路4 Ω，i参考向上；第二支路为3 A向上源；第三支路为电压5i的受控电压源（上负下正）串联2 Ω；最右支路12 Ω。The upper and lower nodes are common. The left branch is 4 Ω with i upward. The next is a 3 A upward source. The third has a controlled voltage source 5i, negative at the top and positive at the bottom, in series with 2 Ω. The right branch is 12 Ω.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

节点法；电流控制电压源；控制电流的参考方向。Nodal analysis, current-controlled voltage sources and current reference direction.
全电路只有一个公共上节点电位，但受控源与2 Ω之间还要写一个电压约束。i向上，因此i=−v/4。There is one common upper-node potential, with an additional constraint at the junction of the controlled source and 2 Ω. Since i points upward, i = −v/4.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

定义节点并写控制关系Define potentials and the control relation

底节点为0 V，上节点为v，受控源下端与2 Ω上端连接点为vx。左支路i向上；受控源正端在下方。Set the bottom to 0 V, the upper potential to v and the source/resistor junction to vx. Current i is upward, and the controlled source is positive at its lower terminal.

i=−v4,vx−v=5i⇒vx=v+5i

上节点列KCLApply KCL at the upper node

三条非独立电流源支路的向下电流分别为v/4、vx/2和v/12，它们之和等于3 A向上注入。The downward currents in the three other branches are v/4, vx/2 and v/12. Their sum equals the 3 A upward injection.

v4+v+5i2+v12=3

代入控制关系并求解Substitute and solve

代入i=−v/4后，vx=−v/4，KCL化为5v/24=3。故v=14.4 V，i=−3.6 A。Substituting i = −v/4 gives vx = −v/4. KCL becomes 5v/24 = 3, so v = 14.4 V and i = −3.6 A.

v=725=14.4V,i=−185=−3.6A

i=−3.6 A，即4 Ω支路实际电流3.6 A向下。i = −3.6 A: the actual current through 4 Ω is 3.6 A downward.

易错点Common errors

  • 原图受控源上负下正，必须写vx−v=5i，不能写v−vx=5i。The source is negative at the top and positive below: write vx−v = 5i, not v−vx = 5i.
  • 负的i同时影响受控源电压，不应在代入时去掉负号。The signed current also controls the source voltage; do not discard its negative sign.

核验依据Verification

独立支路与功率核验：vx=−3.6 V，中间支路向下电流−1.8 A；上节点向下电流3.6−1.8+1.2=3 A。电阻耗散51.84+6.48+17.28=75.6 W；3 A源吸收−43.2 W，受控源上到下电压18 V、向下电流−1.8 A，吸收−32.4 W，总和0。书第333页/PDF342页2-12为−3.6 A。Independent branch/power check: vx = −3.6 V and the middle downward current is −1.8 A. Downward currents sum to 3.6−1.8+1.2 = 3 A. Resistor powers total 51.84+6.48+17.28 = 75.6 W. The 3 A source absorbs −43.2 W; the controlled source has an 18 V top-to-bottom drop with −1.8 A downward, absorbing −32.4 W. Total power is zero. Answer 2-12 on printed p. 333/PDF p. 342 is −3.6 A.

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第63页／PDF第72页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 63 / PDF p. 72. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：2-20Assigned requirement: 2-20

网孔法遇电流源：超网孔加电流约束A current source in mesh analysis: supermesh plus constraint

题目原文Transcribed question

试用网孔分析法求题图2-18所示电路中的电流i₁、i₂和i₃。Use mesh analysis to find currents i₁, i₂ and i₃ in Fig. 2-18.

底部公共节点，左2 V源上负下正，经上方4 Ω接顶中节点，4 Ω电流i₁向右；顶中经5 Ω接右30 V源上端，i₂向右，30 V源也上负下正。顶中与底部之间另有2 Ω电阻（i₃向下）和3 A向下电流源两条并联支路。The bottom is common. The left 2 V source is negative at the top and connects through upper 4 Ω to the middle-top node, with i₁ rightward. A 5 Ω resistor carries i₂ rightward from this node to the upper terminal of the right 30 V source, also negative at the top. A 2 Ω resistor with i₃ downward and a 3 A downward current source separately connect middle-top to bottom.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

顺时针网孔电流；共享支路电流差；超网孔。Clockwise mesh currents, differences in shared branches and supermeshes.
电流源位于两个相邻网孔的公共边，其端电压未知，不能写成电阻压降。把这两个网孔合成超网孔，再补电流源约束。The current source lies between adjacent meshes and has an unknown voltage. Form a supermesh around those meshes and add the current constraint.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

定义三个网孔电流Define three mesh currents

从左到右，三个最小网孔取顺时针电流j₁、j₂、j₃。则i₁=j₁，i₂=j₃，2 Ω中向下电流i₃=j₁−j₂。Take clockwise mesh currents j₁, j₂ and j₃ from left to right. Then i₁ = j₁, i₂ = j₃ and the downward 2 Ω current is i₃ = j₁−j₂.

i1=j1,i2=j3,i3=j1−j2

左网孔KVLLeft-mesh KVL

左2 V源从下向上经过是从正端到负端，为2 V压降。Traversing the left 2 V source upward goes from positive to negative, producing a 2 V drop.

2+4j1+2(j1−j2)=0⇒6j1−2j2=−2

中右超网孔与电流约束Middle/right supermesh and current constraint

超网孔绕过3 A源，经过5 Ω、30 V源和2 Ω。右30 V源从上向下是电压上升。3 A向下等于中网孔向下减右网孔向上。The supermesh bypasses the 3 A source and passes through 5 Ω, the 30 V source and 2 Ω. Traversing the 30 V source downward is a voltage rise. The downward 3 A equals the middle mesh current minus the right mesh current.

5j3−30−2(j1−j2)=0,j2−j3=3

联立并还原支路电流Solve and recover branch currents

解得j₁=2 A、j₂=7 A、j₃=4 A。因此i₁=2 A、i₂=4 A、i₃=−5 A；i₃实际向上5 A。Solving gives j₁ = 2 A, j₂ = 7 A and j₃ = 4 A. Thus i₁ = 2 A, i₂ = 4 A and i₃ = −5 A; its actual direction is 5 A upward.

i1=2A,i2=4A,i3=−5A

i₁=2 A，i₂=4 A，i₃=−5 A（按图示参考方向）。i₁ = 2 A, i₂ = 4 A and i₃ = −5 A, using the figure reference directions.

易错点Common errors

  • i₃是共享支路电流差，不是某一个网孔电流。i₃ is a difference of mesh currents, not a single mesh current.
  • 两个电压源均上负下正，绕行时必须逐个判定电压升降。Both voltage sources are negative at the top; determine each voltage rise or drop from the traversal direction.

核验依据Verification

独立节点法：取底部0 V，则左端−2 V、右端−30 V。顶中节点V满足(V+2)/4+V/2+(V+30)/5+3=0，得V=−10 V。于是i₁=[−2−(−10)]/4=2 A、i₂=[−10−(−30)]/5=4 A、i₃=−10/2=−5 A；KCL也有2=4−5+3。书第333页/PDF342页2-20三项完全相同。Independent nodal check: with bottom at 0 V, the left and right upper terminals are −2 V and −30 V. The middle-top V satisfies (V+2)/4 + V/2 + (V+30)/5 + 3 = 0, giving V = −10 V. Then i₁ = [−2−(−10)]/4 = 2 A, i₂ = [−10−(−30)]/5 = 4 A and i₃ = −10/2 = −5 A. KCL gives 2 = 4−5+3. Answer 2-20 on printed p. 333/PDF p. 342 matches all three.

2-21指定作业Assigned已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第63页／PDF第72页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 63 / PDF p. 72. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：2-21Assigned requirement: 2-21

网孔法中的受控电压源与电流源约束Mesh analysis with controlled voltage and current-source constraints

题目原文Transcribed question

试用网孔分析法求题图2-19所示电路中的电流i。Use mesh analysis to find current i in Fig. 2-19.

左、右上节点由顶端导线直接相连，顶导线电流i₁向右。中间横支路依次为2 Ω和1 Ω，中间节点经3 A向上的电流源接底节点。左侧10 V源、右侧受控电压源2i₁均上正下负，接在上节点与底节点间。所求i在左下导线上向左。The upper left and upper right nodes are directly joined by a top wire carrying i₁ rightward. A middle path contains 2 Ω then 1 Ω; their junction connects to the bottom through a 3 A upward current source. The left 10 V source and right controlled voltage source 2i₁ are both positive at the top. The requested i points left along the lower-left wire.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

网孔电流与共享支路；超网孔；受控源控制关系。Mesh currents and shared branches, supermeshes, and controlled-source relations.
顶导线压降为0，却仍可能有非零电流。受控源的控制量就是这条导线电流i₁；遇到3 A源，要加电流差约束而不是给它指定电压。The top wire has zero voltage drop but can carry nonzero current. Its current i₁ controls the voltage source. The 3 A source supplies a mesh-current difference constraint, not a specified voltage.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

定义网孔与支路电流Define mesh and branch currents

上方、左下、右下三个最小网孔分别取顺时针电流j₁、j₂、j₃。顶导线只有上网孔经过，所以i₁=j₁；左下导线只有左下网孔经过，所以i=j₂。3 A向上对应j₃−j₂。Assign clockwise currents j₁, j₂ and j₃ to the upper, lower-left and lower-right meshes. Only j₁ traverses the top wire, so i₁ = j₁. Only j₂ traverses the lower-left wire, so i = j₂. The upward 3 A is j₃−j₂.

i1=j1,i=j2,j3−j2=3

上网孔列KVLApply KVL to the upper mesh

上网孔顺时针从顶导线到右端，再经1 Ω、2 Ω返回左端。两段电阻电流均为相邻网孔电流之差。Traverse the upper mesh clockwise along the top wire, then return through 1 Ω and 2 Ω. Each resistor current is the difference of adjacent mesh currents.

(j1−j3)+2(j1−j2)=0⇒3j1−2j2−j3=0

列下方超网孔Write the lower supermesh equation

绕过3 A源，沿左下和右下网孔的外边界：依次经过2 Ω、1 Ω、受控电压源，再经10 V源从负到正。受控源压降必须保留为2j₁。Bypass the 3 A source and traverse the lower supermesh boundary through 2 Ω, 1 Ω, the controlled voltage source, then the 10 V source from negative to positive. Retain the controlled-source drop as 2j₁.

2(j2−j1)+(j3−j1)+2j1−10=0⇒−j1+2j2+j3=10

联立求解Solve the system

将前两条KVL相加可得2j₁=10，故j₁=5 A；再用电流源约束和上网孔方程解得j₂=4 A、j₃=7 A。Adding the two KVL equations gives 2j₁ = 10 and j₁ = 5 A. Using the current-source constraint and upper-mesh equation then gives j₂ = 4 A and j₃ = 7 A.

(j1,j2,j3)=(5,4,7)A,i=4A

i=4 A，方向与图中向左的参考方向相同。i = 4 A, in the indicated leftward direction.

易错点Common errors

  • 理想导线电压为0并不意味着电流为0。Zero voltage across an ideal wire does not imply zero current.
  • 受控源不能在写超网孔时去掉；其控制量是顶导线电流，不是所求i。Keep the controlled source in the supermesh. Its control current is the top-wire current, not the requested i.

核验依据Verification

独立节点/功率核验：底节点取0 V，左右上节点均为10 V；中间节点KCL给出(V−10)/2+(V−10)=3，得V=12 V。顶导线i₁=10/2=5 A，左节点有中间流入1 A、顶导线流出5 A，所以10 V源向上4 A，即i=4 A。电阻耗散6 W；10 V源、受控源和3 A源吸收功率依次−40、70、−36 W，总和0。精确有理数MNA复核相同。书第333页/PDF342页2-21为4 A。Independent nodal/power check: set the bottom to 0 V. Both upper nodes are 10 V; middle-node KCL gives (V−10)/2 + (V−10) = 3, so V = 12 V. The controlled relation gives i₁ = 10/2 = 5 A. At the left node, 1 A enters from the middle and 5 A leaves along the wire; the 10 V source therefore supplies 4 A upward, equal to i. Resistors dissipate 6 W; the 10 V, controlled and 3 A sources absorb −40, 70 and −36 W, totaling zero. Exact-rational MNA agrees. Answer 2-21 on printed p. 333/PDF p. 342 is 4 A.

3-4指定作业Assigned已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第87页／PDF第96页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 87 / PDF p. 96. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：3-4Assigned requirement: 3-4

叠加电流，再用总电流计算功率Superpose currents, then use the total current for power

题目原文Transcribed question

利用叠加定理求题图3-4所示电路中的电流i及电流i所在支路电阻消耗的功率。Use superposition to find current i in Fig. 3-4 and the power dissipated by the resistor in its branch.

左4 V源上正下负，顶端经2 Ω到节点A。A经竖直2 Ω接左下节点，i向下；A经顶端10 Ω到节点B。B经竖直10 Ω接右下节点C，另有4 A源从C向上到B。左下节点经2 A向右的电流源到C。The left 4 V source is positive at the top and connects through 2 Ω to node A. A connects through vertical 2 Ω to the lower-left node, with i downward, and through upper 10 Ω to node B. B connects through vertical 10 Ω to lower-right node C, in parallel with a 4 A source directed from C to B. A 2 A current source points from the lower-left node to C.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

叠加定理；独立电压源置零短路，独立电流源置零开路；电阻功率。Superposition; short a zeroed independent voltage source and open a zeroed current source; resistor power.
本题有三个独立源，应分别求三个带符号电流贡献。右侧4 A源单独作用时，电流只在其并联10 Ω支路形成局部回路，对所求i的贡献为0。There are three independent sources, so find three signed current contributions. With only the right 4 A source active, its current circulates through the parallel 10 Ω resistor and contributes zero to i.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

4 V源单独作用The 4 V source acting alone

将2 A、4 A源开路。右侧两只10 Ω形成通向悬空节点的支路，无电流。左侧两个2 Ω串联，所求支路向下电流为1 A。Open the 2 A and 4 A sources. The right pair of 10 Ω resistors leads to a floating node and carries no current. The two left 2 Ω resistors are in series, giving 1 A downward in the requested branch.

i(4V)=42+2=1A

2 A源单独作用The 2 A source acting alone

将4 V源短路、4 A源开路。2 A从右侧经两只10 Ω流到节点A，再经过左侧两个并联2 Ω返回左下节点。两只2 Ω均分电流，所求向下电流为1 A。Short the 4 V source and open the 4 A source. The 2 A current returns from the right through both 10 Ω resistors to A, then through the two parallel left 2 Ω resistors. They share equally, giving 1 A downward in the requested branch.

i(2A)=222+2=1A

4 A源单独作用The 4 A source acting alone

将4 V源短路、2 A源开路。右下节点只连接4 A源与其并联10 Ω，4 A由该10 Ω返回。顶端10 Ω中无电流，因此所求i的贡献为0。Short the 4 V source and open the 2 A source. The lower-right node connects only to the 4 A source and its parallel 10 Ω resistor, which returns all 4 A. The upper 10 Ω carries no current, so this contribution to i is zero.

i(4A)=0

叠加后计算功率Add currents before calculating power

三个贡献按图示参考方向相加。功率必须由总电流求得，不能把各独立作用下的功率相加。Add the three contributions with their signs. Compute power from the total current; powers for separate source excitations do not superpose.

i=1+1+0=2A,P=i2(2Ω)=8W

i=2 A（向下）；该2 Ω电阻消耗8 W。i = 2 A downward; the 2 Ω resistor dissipates 8 W.

易错点Common errors

  • 功率不是源激励的线性函数，1²×2+1²×2=4 W并非正确总功率。Power is nonlinear in source excitation; 1²×2 + 1²×2 = 4 W is not the total power.
  • 电流源置零应开路，不是短路。A zeroed ideal current source is an open circuit.

核验依据Verification

独立完整电路节点法：左下取0 V，左上4 V，设A、B、C为图示节点；右侧KCL给出B−A=20 V、C−B=−20 V，A点KCL为(A−4)/2+A/2+(A−B)/10=0，得A=4、B=24、C=4 V，故i=2 A。电阻总耗散88 W，2 A与4 A源分别提供8 W、80 W，4 V源电流0，功率平衡。精确MNA同时复核三个分量为1、1、0 A。书第334页/PDF343页3-4为2 A、8 W。Independent full-circuit nodal check: set the lower-left node to 0 V and upper-left to 4 V. Right-side KCL gives B−A = 20 V and C−B = −20 V. At A, (A−4)/2 + A/2 + (A−B)/10 = 0, yielding A = 4, B = 24 and C = 4 V, hence i = 2 A. Resistors dissipate 88 W; the 2 A and 4 A sources supply 8 and 80 W, while the 4 V source carries zero current. Exact MNA also verifies the three contributions as 1, 1 and 0 A. Answer 3-4 on printed p. 334/PDF p. 343 is 2 A and 8 W.

3-10指定作业Assigned已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第87页／PDF第96页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 87 / PDF p. 96. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：3-10Assigned requirement: 3-10

替代定理：把指定电流的电阻换成电流源Substitution theorem: replace a specified-current resistor by a source

题目原文Transcribed question

电路如题图3-9所示，欲使支路电流I=2 A，试用替代定理求电阻R的值。For the circuit in Fig. 3-9, use the substitution theorem to find R such that branch current I = 2 A.

左12 V源上正下负，经上方3 Ω到节点A；A经6 Ω接底部公共节点。A到节点B之间有1 Ω及向右4 A源并联。B经3 Ω和待求R分别接底部；R中电流I向下。题干在第87页，原图在第88页。The left 12 V source is positive at the top and connects through upper 3 Ω to A. A connects through 6 Ω to the common bottom node. Between A and B are parallel 1 Ω and rightward 4 A branches. B connects to the bottom through 3 Ω and unknown R; I points downward through R. The statement is on printed p. 87 and the figure on p. 88.

本题图在书第88页／PDF第97页。The source figure is on printed p. 88 / PDF p. 97.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

替代定理；节点KCL；欧姆定律。Substitution theorem, nodal KCL and Ohm’s law.
已知R中应通过2 A，就用同方向2 A理想电流源替代R，先求这条支路需要的端电压，再用R=U/I还原。Since R must carry 2 A, replace it by a 2 A source in the same direction. Find the required branch voltage, then recover R = U/I.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

替代并定义节点Substitute and define nodes

取底部为0 V，左源上端12 V。用B向下到地的2 A源替代R，设其上端电位为vB，左侧节点为vA。Take the bottom as 0 V and the left source top as 12 V. Replace R by a 2 A source directed from B to ground, with upper potential vB. Let the other internal potential be vA.

对A列KCLWrite KCL at A

取离开A为正。包括经3 Ω、6 Ω、1 Ω的电流以及向B流出的4 A。Take currents leaving A as positive, including the 3 Ω, 6 Ω and 1 Ω paths and the 4 A source toward B.

vA−123+vA6+(vA−vB)+4=0⇒32vA−vB=0

对B列KCL并求电压Write KCL at B and solve for voltage

B处4 A流入，替代源2 A流出，另外有1 Ω和3 Ω支路。联立两式得到vA=2 V、vB=3 V。At B, 4 A enters and the substituted 2 A leaves, alongside the 1 Ω and 3 Ω branches. The two equations give vA = 2 V and vB = 3 V.

(vB−vA)+vB3+2−4=0⇒−vA+43vB=2

还原电阻Recover the resistance

R的上正下负端电压为3 V，向下电流为2 A，二者关联，故R为正。R has a 3 V top-positive voltage and a 2 A downward current, using passive references, so R is positive.

R=vBI=32Ω=1.5Ω

R=1.5 Ω。R = 1.5 Ω.

易错点Common errors

  • 替代支路的方向必须与指定I一致；替代后求出的电压是R的端电压。The substituted source must follow the specified I direction; its resulting voltage is the original resistor voltage.
  • 替代定理不是“将电阻删除”，也不要求改变其他电源。Substitution does not simply delete the resistor or change the other sources.

核验依据Verification

独立回代原电路：将1.5 Ω接回后，B=3 V时R向下2 A，旁路3 Ω向下1 A，1 Ω向左1 A，合计4 A正好等于源流入；A处(2−12)/3+2/6+(2−3)+4=0。原电路精确有理数MNA也给出A=2、B=3 V。电阻共耗散44 W，12 V源提供40 W、4 A源提供4 W，平衡。书第334页/PDF343页3-10为1.5 Ω。Independent original-circuit check: restore 1.5 Ω. At B = 3 V, R carries 2 A down, the parallel 3 Ω carries 1 A down, and the 1 Ω carries 1 A left, matching the incoming 4 A. At A, (2−12)/3 + 2/6 + (2−3) + 4 = 0. Exact-rational MNA of the restored circuit gives A = 2 and B = 3 V. Resistors dissipate 44 W; the 12 V and 4 A sources supply 40 and 4 W. Answer 3-10 on printed p. 334/PDF p. 343 is 1.5 Ω.

3-16指定作业Assigned已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第88页／PDF第97页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 88 / PDF p. 97. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：3-16Assigned requirement: 3-16

含受控源的戴维南等效：开路电压加测试源Thévenin equivalent with a controlled source: open circuit plus test source

题目原文Transcribed question

求题图3-15所示单口网络的戴维南等效电路。Find the Thévenin equivalent of the one-port network in Fig. 3-15.

4 V独立源上正下负，上端经2 Ω到中间节点c；c经竖直2 Ω接底节点b，竖直电阻电压U上正下负。c经受控电压源3U到端口a，受控源左负右正。b为端口下端。The 4 V independent source is positive at the top and connects through 2 Ω to middle node c. A vertical 2 Ω joins c to bottom terminal b, with U positive at c. A controlled voltage source 3U connects c to terminal a, negative on the left and positive on the right.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

戴维南等效；开路电压；含受控源网络的外加测试源法。Thévenin equivalent, open-circuit voltage and test-source resistance for controlled-source networks.
受控源右正左负意味着vₐ−U=3U，即端口电压是4U。求等效电阻时只将4 V独立源短路，受控源及其控制关系必须保留。The controlled source is positive on the right, so vₐ−U = 3U and the port voltage is 4U. For equivalent resistance, short only the independent 4 V source and retain the controlled source and its law.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

开路求电压Find the open-circuit voltage

端口a开路，受控源中电流为0。两个2 Ω对4 V等分，故U=2 V；端口电压在此基础上再升高3U。With a open, no current flows through the controlled source. The two 2 Ω resistors divide 4 V equally, giving U = 2 V. The port voltage rises by a further 3U.

Uth=U+3U=4(2)=8V

独立源置零，保留受控源Zero the independent source and retain the dependent source

将4 V源短路后，中间节点c到b有两个2 Ω并联。在a、b间外加上正下负的测试电压Vₜ，由受控源关系有Vₜ=4U。After shorting the 4 V source, two 2 Ω resistors connect c to b in parallel. Apply a top-positive test voltage Vₜ across a,b. The controlled relation remains Vₜ = 4U.

U=Vt4

求流入网络的测试电流Find test current entering the network

从a流入的测试电流经受控源到c，再通过两个2 Ω流出，所以Iₜ=U/2+U/2。The test current entering a passes through the controlled source to c, then through both 2 Ω resistors.

It=U2+U2=U=Vt4,Rth=VtIt=4Ω

画出等效关系State the equivalent relation

戴维南电压源8 V正端朝a，与4 Ω串联。若用从a向外流出的负载电流I_L表示端口特性，电压随电流以4 Ω的斜率下降。The equivalent is an 8 V source, positive toward a, in series with 4 Ω. For load current I_L leaving a, port voltage falls with slope 4 Ω.

vab=8−4IL

戴维南等效：8 V电压源（a端正）串联4 Ω。Thévenin equivalent: an 8 V source, positive toward a, in series with 4 Ω.

易错点Common errors

  • 受控源不是独立源，求Rth时不能把它短路。A controlled source is not an independent source; do not short it when finding Rth.
  • 受控源极性右正左负，端口电压是U+3U，不是U−3U。The dependent source is positive on the right, making the port voltage U+3U, not U−3U.

核验依据Verification

独立完整端口方程：设I为从a流入网络的电流，c节点KCL为(U−4)/2+U/2−I=0，得U=2+I，故vab=4U=8+4I；改用流出电流I_L=−I，得到8−4I_L。另以精确MNA分别检验开路8 V、独立源置零后1 V测试电压对应1/4 A流入、端口短路电流2 A流出，均与Rth=8/2=4 Ω一致。书第334页/PDF343页3-16为8 V、4 Ω。Independent full port law: let I enter the network at a. KCL at c gives (U−4)/2 + U/2 − I = 0, hence U = 2+I and vab = 4U = 8+4I. With outward I_L = −I, this becomes 8−4I_L. Exact MNA separately gives 8 V open circuit, 1/4 A entering under a 1 V test after independent-source suppression, and a 2 A short-circuit current outward, all consistent with Rth = 8/2 = 4 Ω. Answer 3-16 on printed p. 334/PDF p. 343 is 8 V and 4 Ω.

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第88页／PDF第97页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 88 / PDF p. 97. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：3-17Assigned requirement: 3-17

受控源诺顿等效：短路电流与测试电阻Norton equivalent with a controlled source: short-circuit current and test resistance

题目原文Transcribed question

求题图3-16所示单口网络的诺顿等效电路。Find the Norton equivalent of the one-port network in Fig. 3-16.

左20 V源上正下负，顶端经2 Ω到端口a，2 Ω电压u₁左正右负。a向下先经上正下负的受控电压源2u₁，再经4 Ω接底端b。The left 20 V source is positive at the top and connects through 2 Ω to terminal a. The 2 Ω voltage u₁ is positive on the left. A downward branch from a contains a controlled voltage source 2u₁, positive at the top, followed by 4 Ω to bottom terminal b.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

诺顿等效；短路电流；受控源网络测试法。Norton equivalent, short-circuit current and test-source analysis with dependent sources.
端口短路时u₁=20 V，受控源电压仍为40 V，并不会一起变成0。它使4 Ω上端为−40 V，因此该支路实际向端口提供电流。Shorting the port gives u₁ = 20 V, so the controlled source remains at 40 V. It places the 4 Ω upper terminal at −40 V, making this branch supply current toward the port.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

短路求诺顿电流Short the port to find Norton current

将a、b短路，取b为0 V，故a也为0 V。左2 Ω向a送入(20−0)/2=10 A。受控源2u₁=40 V，使其下端c为−40 V，4 Ω中向下电流−10 A，即向a再送入10 A。Short a,b and set b to 0 V, so a is also 0 V. The left 2 Ω sends (20−0)/2 = 10 A into a. The controlled source is 2u₁ = 40 V, placing its lower node c at −40 V. The downward 4 Ω current is −10 A, supplying another 10 A to a.

Isc=10+10=20A

置零独立源并加测试电压Suppress the independent source and apply test voltage

仅把20 V源短路，在端口加Vₜ。此时u₁=−Vₜ，受控源关系Vₜ−v_c=2u₁=−2Vₜ，故v_c=3Vₜ。Short only the 20 V source and apply Vₜ at the port. Then u₁ = −Vₜ and the controlled law is Vₜ−v_c = 2u₁ = −2Vₜ, so v_c = 3Vₜ.

vc=3Vt

求诺顿电阻与方向Find Norton resistance and source direction

流入端口的测试电流分为经2 Ω到地与经受控源、4 Ω到地两部分。诺顿源方向从b向a，才能使外部短路电流从a流向b。The entering test current splits between the 2 Ω path and the controlled-source/4 Ω path. The Norton source points from b to a, giving an external short-circuit current from a to b.

It=Vt2+3Vt4=54Vt,RN=VtIt=45Ω=0.8Ω

诺顿等效：20 A电流源（从b向a）并联0.8 Ω。Norton equivalent: a 20 A source from b to a in parallel with 0.8 Ω.

易错点Common errors

  • a、b短路只令端口电压为0，不令受控源的控制电压u₁为0。Shorting a,b zeros the port voltage, not the controlling voltage u₁.
  • 测试时u₁的参考极性左正右负，因此u₁=−Vₜ。During the test, u₁ is positive on the left, so u₁ = −Vₜ.

核验依据Verification

独立完整端口法：端口电压v时u₁=20−v，受控源下节点vc=v−2(20−v)=3v−40。流入网络的电流I=(v−20)/2+(3v−40)/4=5v/4−20，故向外电流为20−v/0.8。开路v=16 V，16/20=0.8 Ω。精确MNA以0.8 Ω负载检验得v=8 V、负载电流10 A；总电阻耗散216 W，两个源提供216 W，功率平衡。书第334页/PDF343页3-17为20 A、0.8 Ω。Independent full port law: at voltage v, u₁ = 20−v and the lower controlled-source node is vc = v−2(20−v) = 3v−40. Entering current is I = (v−20)/2 + (3v−40)/4 = 5v/4−20, so outward current is 20−v/0.8. Open circuit gives v = 16 V and 16/20 = 0.8 Ω. Exact MNA with a 0.8 Ω load gives 8 V and 10 A; resistors dissipate 216 W, exactly supplied by the two sources. Answer 3-17 on printed p. 334/PDF p. 343 is 20 A and 0.8 Ω.
