3-19指定作业Assigned已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第88页／PDF第97页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 88 / PDF p. 97. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：3-19Assigned requirement: 3-19

戴维南法：先移开负载，再求端口等效Thévenin method: remove the load before finding the port equivalent

题目原文Transcribed question

应用戴维南定理求题图3-18所示电路中的电流i。Use Thévenin’s theorem to find current i in Fig. 3-18.

底部公共节点。左节点A经1 Ω接底部，A到中节点B有24 V源，左正右负；B经上正下负的4 V源接底部。A到右节点C经顶端5 Ω，B到C经2 Ω；C经右侧4 Ω负载接底部，电流i向下。The bottom is common. Left node A connects through 1 Ω to the bottom. A 24 V source from A to middle node B is positive on the left. B connects through a top-positive 4 V source to the bottom. A connects to right node C through upper 5 Ω; B connects to C through 2 Ω. The right 4 Ω load joins C to the bottom, with i downward.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

戴维南定理；开路端口KCL；独立电压源置零短路。Thévenin’s theorem, open-port KCL and suppression of independent voltage sources.
将右侧4 Ω视为负载移开。两个理想源先固定B=4 V、A=28 V，左1 Ω只影响源电流，不改变这些节点电位。Remove the right 4 Ω load. The ideal sources fix B = 4 V and A = 28 V. The left 1 Ω affects source currents but cannot change those fixed potentials.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

负载开路求UthRemove the load and find Uth

取底部0 V，B=4 V，A−B=24 V，所以A=28 V。设负载开路后C点电位为Uth，对C列KCL。Take the bottom as 0 V. Then B = 4 V and A−B = 24 V, so A = 28 V. Let the open-circuit potential at C be Uth and apply KCL.

Uth−285+Uth−42=0⇒Uth=767V

独立源置零求RthSuppress independent sources to find Rth

将24 V、4 V源均短路，A、B与底节点合并，1 Ω被短路。端口C到地只剩5 Ω与2 Ω并联。Short the 24 V and 4 V sources. A, B and the bottom merge, bypassing the 1 Ω resistor. The port sees only 5 Ω in parallel with 2 Ω.

Rth=5∥2=107Ω

接回4 Ω负载Reconnect the 4 Ω load

戴维南源与Rth、负载串联；电压源正端朝C，因此负载电流向下为正。The Thévenin source, Rth and load are in series. The source is positive toward C, giving positive downward load current.

i=UthRth+4=76/710/7+4=2A

i=2 A，沿右侧4 Ω向下。i = 2 A downward through the right 4 Ω resistor.

易错点Common errors

  • 开路时移去的是右侧4 Ω负载，不是中间2 Ω。Remove the right 4 Ω load, not the middle 2 Ω resistor.
  • 24 V源左正右负，因此A=B+24，不是B−24。The 24 V source is positive on the left, so A = B+24, not B−24.

核验依据Verification

独立直接节点法：接回负载后C满足(C−28)/5+(C−4)/2+C/4=0，得C=8 V，i=8/4=2 A。C点流入4 A经5 Ω，流出2 A经2 Ω、2 A经4 Ω，KCL成立。精确MNA同时复核开路76/7 V与接载8 V。完整电路电阻耗散888 W，24 V、4 V源分别提供768 W、120 W，功率平衡。书第334页/PDF343页3-19为2 A。Independent direct nodal check: with the load restored, (C−28)/5 + (C−4)/2 + C/4 = 0 gives C = 8 V and i = 8/4 = 2 A. At C, 4 A enters through 5 Ω and 2 A leaves through each of the 2 Ω and 4 Ω branches. Exact MNA also verifies the open-circuit 76/7 V and loaded 8 V. Resistors dissipate 888 W, supplied as 768 W and 120 W by the 24 V and 4 V sources. Answer 3-19 on printed p. 334/PDF p. 343 is 2 A.

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第89页／PDF第98页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 89 / PDF p. 98. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：3-22Assigned requirement: 3-22

由两次负载读数反推单口网络Identify a one-port from two loaded measurements

题目原文Transcribed question

题图3-21所示电路中，N为线性含源电阻网络。已知当开关S₁、S₂均打开时，电压表的读数为6 V；当开关S₁闭合、S₂打开时，电压表的读数为4 V。试求当开关S₁打开、S₂闭合时电压表的读数。In Fig. 3-21, N is a linear resistive network containing sources. The voltmeter reads 6 V with S₁ and S₂ both open, and 4 V with S₁ closed and S₂ open. Find its reading with S₁ open and S₂ closed.

网络N的上下端口外接永久6 Ω并联支路；另有4 Ω串S₁、2 Ω串S₂两条可切换并联支路。右侧电压表跨接端口，上正下负，按理想电压表处理。Across N are a permanent 6 Ω branch, a 4 Ω branch in series with S₁, and a 2 Ω branch in series with S₂. The voltmeter is across the port, positive at the top, and is treated as ideal.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

线性含源单口的戴维南模型；负载并联；电压分配。Thévenin modeling of a linear source-containing one-port, parallel loads and voltage division.
两个开关打开时6 Ω仍接在端口，6 V不是N的开路电压。用两次“端电压、总负载电流”测量建立U=Uth−RthI，再预测第三种负载。The 6 Ω remains connected with both switches open, so 6 V is not the open-circuit voltage of N. Use the two voltage/current measurements in U = Uth−Rth I, then predict the third load.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

第一状态给出一条端口方程Form the first port equation

S₁、S₂均打开，负载只有6 Ω。理想电压表不取电流，因此N向外供电流6/6=1 A。With both switches open, the only load is 6 Ω. The ideal voltmeter draws no current, so N supplies 6/6 = 1 A.

6=Uth−Rth(1)

第二状态给出另一条方程Form the second port equation

S₁闭合、S₂打开，负载为6 Ω与4 Ω并联。端电压4 V时总电流4/6+4/4=5/3 A。With S₁ closed and S₂ open, 6 Ω and 4 Ω are parallel. At 4 V, total load current is 4/6 + 4/4 = 5/3 A.

4=Uth−Rth53

求N的等效参数Identify N’s equivalent parameters

两式相减得到2=(2/3)Rth，再回代第一式。Subtract the equations to get 2 = (2/3)Rth, then substitute into the first equation.

Rth=3Ω,Uth=9V

预测第三状态Predict the third state

S₁打开、S₂闭合时负载为6 Ω与2 Ω并联，即1.5 Ω。用戴维南电压分配。With S₁ open and S₂ closed, the load is 6 Ω in parallel with 2 Ω, or 1.5 Ω. Apply voltage division to the identified Thévenin circuit.

RL=6∥2=1.5Ω,U=91.53+1.5=3V

电压表读数为3 V（上正下负）。The voltmeter reads 3 V, positive at the top.

易错点Common errors

  • 不可把第一状态误认为开路：6 Ω始终接入。Do not treat the first state as an open circuit; the 6 Ω is always connected.
  • 第二状态总电流应包含6 Ω支路和4 Ω支路。The second-state current includes both the 6 Ω and 4 Ω branches.

核验依据Verification

独立等效检验：也可先将固定6 Ω并入N，形成新单口，其开路电压就是6 V；接4 Ω时降为4 V，所以新单口内阻2 Ω。再接2 Ω得到6×2/(2+2)=3 V，与前法相同。原N参数9 V、3 Ω回代两次已知状态分别给6 V、4 V，第三状态供电流0.5+1.5=2 A且9−3×2=3 V。精确MNA三种状态均相符。书第334页/PDF343页3-22为3 V。Independent equivalent check: include the permanent 6 Ω inside an enlarged one-port. Its open-circuit voltage is 6 V; attaching 4 Ω reduces it to 4 V, so its resistance is 2 Ω. Attaching 2 Ω therefore gives 6×2/(2+2) = 3 V. The original 9 V, 3 Ω model reproduces the known 6 V and 4 V states; the third supplies 0.5+1.5 = 2 A and satisfies 9−3×2 = 3 V. Exact MNA verifies all three states. Answer 3-22 on printed p. 334/PDF p. 343 is 3 V.

3-29指定作业Assigned已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第89页／PDF第98页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 89 / PDF p. 98. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：3-29Assigned requirement: 3-29

最大功率与受控源：先求真实端口电阻Maximum power with a controlled source: find the actual port resistance

题目原文Transcribed question

试确定题图3-28所示电路中负载电阻R_L取何值时可获得最大功率。Determine the load resistance R_L that receives maximum power in Fig. 3-28.

左侧15 V独立源上正下负，上端经4 Ω到节点c，4 Ω中控制电流I向右。c经上正下负的受控电压源6I接底部公共节点；c经2 Ω到负载上端a，R_L从a接到底部。The left 15 V independent source is positive at the top and connects through 4 Ω to c, with control current I rightward. A controlled voltage source 6I, positive at c, connects c to the bottom. A 2 Ω resistor joins c to load terminal a, and R_L joins a to the bottom.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

最大功率传输定理；含受控源的戴维南等效；测试源法。Maximum-power transfer, Thévenin equivalents with dependent sources and test-source analysis.
受控电压源直接限定中间节点电位，所以从负载看进去并非4 Ω与2 Ω简单串联。先保留控制关系求Rth，再令正电阻负载RL=Rth。The controlled voltage source fixes the middle-node voltage, so the load does not simply see 4 Ω and 2 Ω in series. Retain its control law to find Rth, then match the positive load resistance to Rth.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

开路求端口电压Find the open-circuit voltage

移去RL，2 Ω中无电流，端口a电位等于c。左回路有4I+6I=15，得到I=1.5 A、c点电位6I=9 V。Remove RL. No current flows through 2 Ω, so a and c have equal potentials. The left loop gives 4I+6I = 15, hence I = 1.5 A and c = 6I = 9 V.

I=154+6=1.5A,Uth=9V

独立源置零，求测试电阻Suppress the independent source and find test resistance

只把15 V源短路，保留6I受控源。设c点电位为vc，则I=(0−vc)/4，同时vc=6I。两式只能给vc=0，因此外加端口测试电压全部落在2 Ω上。Short only the 15 V source and retain the 6I source. If c is at vc, I = (0−vc)/4 while vc = 6I. These force vc = 0, so an applied port test voltage falls entirely across 2 Ω.

vc=6−vc4⇒vc=0,It=Vt2,Rth=2Ω

使用最大功率条件Apply the maximum-power condition

等效内阻为正，电阻负载在RL=Rth时获得最大功率。题目只要求负载值；最大功率可作为额外核验。The equivalent resistance is positive, so the resistive load receives maximum power at RL = Rth. The question asks only for RL; the maximum power is a supplementary check.

RL=Rth=2Ω,Pmax=Uth24Rth=818W

R_L=2 Ω时获得最大功率。Maximum power occurs at R_L = 2 Ω.

易错点Common errors

  • 求内阻时不能将受控源置零，也不能直接写4+2=6 Ω。Do not suppress the controlled source or assume a resistance of 4+2 = 6 Ω.
  • 控制电流I仍按图示从左向右，独立源置零后I=−vc/4，负号不能漏。The control current remains referenced left to right, so after source suppression I = −vc/4.

核验依据Verification

独立完整负载关系：无论RL取何正值，左回路仍给c=9 V，所以P_L=81RL/(RL+2)²，导数为81(2−RL)/(RL+2)³；RL<2时为正，RL>2时为负，唯一最大值在2 Ω。此时负载电压4.5 V、电流2.25 A、功率81/8 W。精确MNA在该负载与多个邻近负载上复核，并检验完整功率平衡：4 Ω、2 Ω、负载耗散9+10.125+10.125 W，两源提供22.5+6.75 W，合计0。书第334页/PDF343页3-29为2 Ω。Independent full load law: for every positive RL, the left loop still fixes c = 9 V, so P_L = 81RL/(RL+2)². Its derivative is 81(2−RL)/(RL+2)³, positive below 2 and negative above 2, giving the unique maximum at 2 Ω. The load then has 4.5 V, 2.25 A and 81/8 W. Exact MNA verifies this load and several neighboring values, plus full power balance: the 4 Ω, 2 Ω and load dissipate 9+10.125+10.125 W, supplied as 22.5+6.75 W by the sources. Answer 3-29 on printed p. 334/PDF p. 343 is 2 Ω.

5-7指定作业Assigned已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第140页／PDF第149页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 140 / PDF p. 149. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：5-7Assigned requirement: 5-7

电容网络分压：先串并联，再回到每只电容Capacitor voltage division: reduce, then recover each voltage

题目原文Transcribed question

试求题图5-5所示电路中各电容上的电压，已知ab端的电压为u_ab=12 V。Find the voltage across each capacitor in Fig. 5-5, given u_ab = 12 V.

C₁=12 μF接a、c；C₂=3 μF接c、b；C₃=4 μF接c、d；C₄=12 μF接d、b。下方b为公共节点。题干在印刷页140，原图在页141。C₁ = 12 μF lies between a,c; C₂ = 3 μF between c,b; C₃ = 4 μF between c,d; and C₄ = 12 μF between d,b. Terminal b is the common bottom node. The statement is on printed p. 140 and figure on p. 141.

本题图在书第141页／PDF第150页。The source figure is on printed p. 141 / PDF p. 150.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

电容串并联；串联电荷相等；u_xy=v_x−v_y。Series/parallel capacitors, equal series charge and u_xy = v_x−v_y.
先看节点：C₃与C₄串联，再与C₂并联，最后与C₁串联。按本题通常约定，中间浮置导体初始净电荷为0；如果预先存有任意净电荷，仅给端电压不能唯一确定各电压。Read the nodes: C₃ and C₄ are series, their equivalent is parallel with C₂, and that group is series with C₁. Use the exercise’s usual assumption of zero initial net charge on floating internal conductors; arbitrary trapped net charge would require additional initial data.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

逐级求等效电容Reduce the capacitor network

C₃与C₄串联得3 μF，与C₂并联后c、b间为6 μF。它再与C₁的12 μF串联。C₃ and C₄ in series give 3 μF; in parallel with C₂ this gives 6 μF between c,b. This is in series with C₁ = 12 μF.

C34=4·124+12=3μF,Ccb=3+3=6μF,Cab=12·612+6=4μF

求第一层分压Find the first voltage division

串联组电荷Q=C_ab×12 V=48 μC，因此C₁电压为4 V，c、b间电压为8 V。C₂直接跨接c、b。The series charge is Q = C_ab×12 V = 48 μC. Thus C₁ has 4 V and the c,b group has 8 V. C₂ is directly across c,b.

uac=4812=4V,ucb=486=8V

求C₃、C₄各自分压Recover C₃ and C₄ voltages

C₃、C₄串联组跨接8 V，其电荷Q₃₄=3 μF×8 V=24 μC。电容越小，在相同电荷下电压越大。The C₃,C₄ series pair is across 8 V, with charge Q₃₄ = 3 μF×8 V = 24 μC. The smaller capacitance has the larger voltage for equal charge.

ucd=244=6V,udb=2412=2V

C₁：u_ac=4 V；C₂：u_cb=8 V；C₃：u_cd=6 V；C₄：u_db=2 V，均按下标顺序取正端、负端。C₁: u_ac = 4 V; C₂: u_cb = 8 V; C₃: u_cd = 6 V; C₄: u_db = 2 V. Each subscript order defines the positive and negative terminals.

易错点Common errors

  • 串联电容按电容量倒数分压，不按容量大小正比分压。Series capacitor voltages vary inversely with capacitance.
  • C₁电荷48 μC不等于C₃的24 μC；中间并联支路会分配电荷。C₁ carries 48 μC while C₃ carries 24 μC; charge splits between the middle parallel branches.

核验依据Verification

采用本章串并联电容的通常条件：各内部孤立导体原先净电荷为0（例如所有电容由未充电状态接入）。如果内部导体预先带有未知净电荷，仅给u_ab不足以唯一决定四个电压。Use the usual condition for the chapter’s series/parallel capacitor model: each internal isolated conductor initially has zero net charge, as when all capacitors are connected uncharged. If unknown trapped charges are present, u_ab alone does not determine the four voltages uniquely.

独立节点电荷核验：取b=0、a=12 V，则c=8、d=2 V。c导体净电荷12(8−12)+3×8+4(8−2)=0 μC；d导体净电荷4(2−8)+12×2=0 μC。总储能为288 μJ，与等效4 μF在12 V下的½CU²相同。书第335页/PDF344页5-7四个电压完全一致。Independent node-charge check: set b = 0 and a = 12 V, giving c = 8 and d = 2 V. The c conductor has net charge 12(8−12)+3×8+4(8−2) = 0 μC; d has 4(2−8)+12×2 = 0 μC. Total energy is 288 μJ, equal to ½CU² for the 4 μF equivalent at 12 V. All four voltages match answer 5-7 on printed p. 335/PDF p. 344.

5-11指定作业Assigned已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第141页／PDF第150页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 141 / PDF p. 150. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：5-11Assigned requirement: 5-11

换路初始值：同时保留电容电压与电感电流Switching initial values: retain capacitor voltage and inductor current

题目原文Transcribed question

题图5-7所示电路原已处于稳态，在t=0时刻开关S打开，求i(0⁺)。The circuit in Fig. 5-7 is initially in steady state. Switch S opens at t = 0. Find i(0⁺).

左4 A向上电流源与5 Ω并联，其上节点经开关S连接1 F电容上端。电容下端接底部公共节点。电容上端经另一5 Ω到右节点，i向右。右节点经0.5 H电感和3 Ω分别接底部，两者并联。A 4 A upward source is parallel with left 5 Ω. Their upper node connects through S to the upper terminal of a 1 F capacitor; its lower terminal is common bottom. Another 5 Ω connects capacitor top to the right node, with i rightward. The right node connects to bottom through parallel 0.5 H and 3 Ω branches.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

直流稳态电容开路、电感短路；换路定则；瞬时节点KCL。Open capacitors and short inductors in DC steady state, switching continuity and instantaneous KCL.
题目只求0⁺瞬间，无需求整个LC暂态。先求0⁻的uC、iL，再在换路后的电路用同值电压源、电流源表示这两个初始状态。Only the instant 0⁺ is requested, so the complete LC transient is unnecessary. Find uC and iL at 0⁻, then impose those states on the post-switch circuit.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

求换路前的状态Find the pre-switch state

S原来闭合。稳态时1 F开路、0.5 H短路，右节点为0 V，3 Ω无电流。两只5 Ω从电流源上节点看去并联，4 A均分。S is initially closed. In steady state, the capacitor is open and inductor shorted. The right node is 0 V and 3 Ω carries no current. The two 5 Ω branches are parallel and share 4 A equally.

uC(0−)=4(5∥5)=10V,iL(0−)=2A

施加换路定则Apply continuity at switching

开关打开只断开左侧激励。没有冲激激励，故电容电压仍10 V、电感电流仍向下2 A。Opening S disconnects the left excitation. With no impulsive excitation, capacitor voltage remains 10 V and downward inductor current remains 2 A.

uC(0+)=10V,iL(0+)=2A

用右节点KCL求电流Use right-node KCL

设右节点瞬时电压v。左边5 Ω送入(10−v)/5，右侧3 Ω与电感流出v/3+2。解得v=0，因此所求电流2 A。Let the instantaneous right-node voltage be v. The 5 Ω supplies (10−v)/5, while the 3 Ω and inductor draw v/3+2. This gives v = 0 and the requested current 2 A.

10−v5=v3+2⇒v=0,i(0+)=105=2A

i(0⁺)=2 A，方向向右。i(0⁺) = 2 A rightward.

易错点Common errors

  • 电容电流和电感电压可以突变；保持连续的是uC和iL。Capacitor current and inductor voltage may jump; uC and iL are the continuous states.
  • 换路后电感不能笼统当短路，必须先用其已知初始电流列方程。本题得到0⁺电压恰好为0，是计算结果。Do not automatically short the inductor after switching. Impose its initial current; its zero voltage at 0⁺ is a result here.

核验依据Verification

独立约束检查：0⁻电流源KCL为10/5+10/5=4 A；0⁺右节点KCL为2=2+0，电容电压、电感电流均满足连续性。换路瞬间总储能为½×1×10²+½×0.5×2²=51 J，切换前后相同，没有状态跳变所需的冲激能量。书第335页/PDF344页5-11为2 A。Independent constraints: before switching, source-node KCL is 10/5+10/5 = 4 A; afterward, right-node KCL is 2 = 2+0 and both states are continuous. Stored energy is ½×1×10² + ½×0.5×2² = 51 J on both sides of switching. Answer 5-11 on printed p. 335/PDF p. 344 is 2 A.

5-15指定作业Assigned已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第141页／PDF第150页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 141 / PDF p. 150. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：5-15Assigned requirement: 5-15

受控源初始充电与断开后的RC放电Controlled-source charging and RC discharge after opening

题目原文Transcribed question

题图5-11所示电路在开关S打开前已达稳态，t=0时刻开关打开，求开关打开后的电容电压u_C(t)。The circuit in Fig. 5-11 is in steady state before switch S opens at t = 0. Find the capacitor voltage u_C(t) after opening.

左10 V源上正下负，向上参考电流i，经S和5 kΩ到节点x。x经上正下负的受控电压源10⁴i接底部，并经20 kΩ到2 μF电容上端。电容下端接底部，uC上正下负。原图明确标20 kΩ与2 μF。The left 10 V source is positive at the top, with upward current i, and connects through S and 5 kΩ to node x. A top-positive controlled voltage source 10⁴i joins x to bottom. A 20 kΩ resistor connects x to the top of a 2 μF capacitor, with uC top-positive. The original figure explicitly labels 20 kΩ and 2 μF.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

受控源关系；电容电压连续性；RC零输入响应；单位换算。Controlled-source relations, capacitor-voltage continuity, RC zero-input response and unit conversion.
先用闭合时的受控关系求初始充电电压。打开后控制电流i因断路而为0，所以受控源电压成为0，电容仅经20 kΩ放电。这里源变为0是控制量计算结果。First find the initial charge using the closed-switch control law. After opening, i is zero because its branch is broken; the controlled voltage becomes zero and the capacitor discharges through 20 kΩ. The dependent source becomes zero as a consequence of its control current.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

换路前求电容电压Find the pre-switch capacitor voltage

稳态电容开路，20 kΩ无电流，因此uC(0⁻)=vx。由左回路和受控源可得i=(10−vx)/5000、vx=10000i。In steady state, the capacitor is open and 20 kΩ carries no current, so uC(0⁻) = vx. The left loop and source law give i = (10−vx)/5000 and vx = 10000i.

vx=2(10−vx)⇒uC(0−)=203V

换路后的三要素Find the three post-switch parameters

电容电压连续；开关打开后i=0，vx=0，最终电压0。电容看入电阻为20 kΩ，时间常数须将μF化为10⁻⁶ F。Capacitor voltage is continuous. After opening, i = 0 and vx = 0; the final voltage is zero. The capacitor sees 20 kΩ. Convert μF to 10⁻⁶ F when calculating the time constant.

uC(0+)=203V,uC(∞)=0,τ=20000(2×10−6)=0.04s

写出正确响应并核对附录State the response and compare the appendix

因此衰减系数为1/τ=25 s⁻¹。原书附录写6.67e⁻²·⁵ᵗ，按本题原图参数指数小了10倍；这里采用满足原电路方程的25 s⁻¹，并明确保留此差异。The decay rate is 1/τ = 25 s⁻¹. The printed appendix gives 6.67e^(−2.5t), whose exponent is ten times too small for the supplied figure. Use 25 s⁻¹, which satisfies the original circuit equation, and retain the discrepancy explicitly.

uC(t)=203e−25tV,t≥0

u_C(t)=(20/3)e^(−25t) V，t≥0，t以秒计。附录的−2.5t与原图参数不符。u_C(t) = (20/3)e^(−25t) V for t≥0 in seconds. The appendix exponent −2.5t is inconsistent with the figure parameters.

易错点Common errors

  • 20 kΩ×2 μF=0.04 s，不是0.4 s。20 kΩ×2 μF = 0.04 s, not 0.4 s.
  • 不得为了匹配附录而改读原图的电容或电阻值。Do not reinterpret the figure values just to match the appendix.

核验依据Verification

独立微分方程核验：2×10⁻⁶ duC/dt+uC/20000=0，代入e⁻²⁵ᵗ时两项严格抵消，代入e⁻²·⁵ᵗ则不成立。储能核验：初始½C(20/3)²=1/22500 J，积分∫₀∞uC²/20000 dt恰为同值，且最终电压0。原图20 kΩ、2 μF及附录第335页/PDF344页的−2.5t已分别放大目视确认。附录初值6.67是20/3的舍入，但指数存在实质不一致。Independent ODE check: 2×10⁻⁶ duC/dt + uC/20000 = 0. Substitution of e^(−25t) cancels exactly, while e^(−2.5t) does not. Energy check: the initial ½C(20/3)² is 1/22500 J, exactly equal to ∫₀∞uC²/20000 dt; final voltage is zero. The figure’s 20 kΩ and 2 μF and the appendix exponent −2.5t on printed p. 335/PDF p. 344 were each visually enlarged and checked. Its 6.67 initial value is ordinary rounding, but the exponent is materially inconsistent.

指定要求：5-24Assigned requirement: 5-24

三种初态下的零输入、零状态与全响应Zero-input, zero-state and full responses for three initial states

题目原文Transcribed question

对题图5-20所示电路。 (1) 若Uₛ=18 V，u_C(0⁻)=−6 V，求电容电压的零输入响应、零状态响应和全响应。 (2) 若Uₛ=18 V，u_C(0⁻)=−12 V，求全响应u_C(t)。 (3) 若Uₛ=36 V，u_C(0⁻)=−6 V，求全响应u_C(t)。For the circuit in Fig. 5-20: (1) With Uₛ = 18 V and u_C(0⁻) = −6 V, find the zero-input, zero-state and full capacitor-voltage responses. (2) With Uₛ = 18 V and u_C(0⁻) = −12 V, find the full u_C(t). (3) With Uₛ = 36 V and u_C(0⁻) = −6 V, find the full u_C(t).

Uₛ独立源上正下负，上端经6 Ω到电容上节点；该节点经3 Ω与1/4 F电容分别接底部公共节点。uC上正下负。The top-positive Uₛ source connects through 6 Ω to the capacitor’s top node. Parallel 3 Ω and 1/4 F branches connect this node to common bottom. uC is positive at the top.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

RC三要素法；零输入响应保留初态；零状态响应初态为0；线性叠加。The three-parameter RC method, retained initial state for zero-input response, zero initial state for zero-state response, and linear superposition.
三问拓扑相同，所以时间常数相同。源大小决定最终值，电容初态决定指数项幅度。第一问先把两个响应写清，再叠加。All three parts share the topology and time constant. The source fixes the final value, while the capacitor initial state fixes the exponential amplitude. In part (1), write both component responses before adding them.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

统一求时间常数和最终值Find the common time constant and final value

独立源置零后，电容看到6 Ω与3 Ω并联为2 Ω。稳态电容开路，3 Ω分得源电压的1/3。With the independent source suppressed, the capacitor sees 6 Ω parallel with 3 Ω, or 2 Ω. In steady state the capacitor is open, and 3 Ω receives one third of the source voltage.

τ=(6∥3)14=12s,uC(∞)=Us3

第一问分别写两个分量Write both components in part (1)

零输入时仅保留−6 V初态；零状态时源18 V有效、初态0，最终值6 V。相加后初态为−6 V、最终值6 V。The zero-input response retains only the −6 V initial state. The zero-state response uses the 18 V source and zero initial state, approaching 6 V. Their sum starts at −6 V and approaches 6 V.

uzi=−6e−2t,uzs=6(1−e−2t),uC=6−12e−2t(V)

第二问改变初态Change the initial state in part (2)

最终值仍6 V，初态改为−12 V，所以指数项系数为−12−6=−18 V。The final value remains 6 V, but the initial value is −12 V. The exponential amplitude is −12−6 = −18 V.

uC(2)(t)=6−18e−2t(V)

第三问改变激励Change the source in part (3)

源36 V使最终值变为12 V，初态仍−6 V，指数项系数为−6−12=−18 V。全部公式适用于t≥0且t以秒计。The 36 V source makes the final value 12 V. The initial value remains −6 V, giving amplitude −6−12 = −18 V. All formulas apply for t≥0 in seconds.

uC(3)(t)=12−18e−2t(V)

(1) 零输入−6e^(−2t) V；零状态6(1−e^(−2t)) V；全响应6−12e^(−2t) V。 (2) 6−18e^(−2t) V。 (3) 12−18e^(−2t) V。(1) Zero input: −6e^(−2t) V; zero state: 6(1−e^(−2t)) V; full: 6−12e^(−2t) V. (2) 6−18e^(−2t) V. (3) 12−18e^(−2t) V.

易错点Common errors

  • 三问初始电压都有负号；第二问是−12 V，不是+12 V。The initial voltages are signed; part (2) has −12 V, not +12 V.
  • 时间常数用源置零后的6 Ω∥3 Ω，不是6+3。Use 6 Ω∥3 Ω after source suppression, not 6+3 Ω, for the time constant.

核验依据Verification

独立原电路方程为¼duC/dt+(uC−Uₛ)/6+uC/3=0，即duC/dt+2uC=(2/3)Uₛ。逐一代入三个全响应，常数项和指数项残差均严格为0；初始值分别−6、−12、−6 V，最终值分别6、6、12 V，初始斜率分别24、36、36 V/s。第一问零输入储能4.5 J，恰等于在等效2 Ω中的总耗散。书第336页/PDF345页5-24全部小问一致。The independent original-circuit equation is ¼duC/dt + (uC−Uₛ)/6 + uC/3 = 0, or duC/dt+2uC = (2/3)Uₛ. Each full response gives exactly zero constant and exponential residuals. Their initial values are −6, −12 and −6 V; final values 6, 6 and 12 V; initial slopes 24, 36 and 36 V/s. The part (1) zero-input energy is 4.5 J, equal to the total loss in equivalent 2 Ω. Every part matches answer 5-24 on printed p. 336/PDF p. 345.

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第143页／PDF第152页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 143 / PDF p. 152. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：5-27Assigned requirement: 5-27

理想电压源将RL与RC暂态分开An ideal voltage source separates RL and RC transients

题目原文Transcribed question

题图5-23所示电路在开关S闭合前已经达到稳态，t=0时刻开关闭合，求开关闭合后的电流i(t)。The circuit in Fig. 5-23 reaches steady state before S closes at t = 0. Find current i(t) after closing.

左支路为25 V源上正下负，向上经5 Ω、再经顶端0.5 H电感到节点A。A经顶端10 Ω到右节点B，B经竖直10 Ω接地；B还经0.2 F电容及上正下负40 V源串联接地。S闭合后把A接到上正下负10 V源上端，所求i(t)沿S和10 V源向下。The left top-positive 25 V source connects upward through 5 Ω and then upper 0.5 H to node A. A connects through upper 10 Ω to B, which connects through vertical 10 Ω to ground. Another B-to-ground branch contains a 0.2 F capacitor followed by a top-positive 40 V source. Closing S connects A to the top of a top-positive 10 V source. Requested i(t) flows down through S and that 10 V source.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

直流稳态；电感电流、电容电压连续；分别求RL、RC响应；节点KCL。DC steady state, inductor-current and capacitor-voltage continuity, separate RL/RC responses and nodal KCL.
开关闭合后A被理想10 V源固定为10 V，使左右动态支路各自成为一阶电路。先分别求电感流入A的电流和A流向右侧的电流，再相减得到开关支路电流。After closing, the ideal 10 V source fixes A at 10 V, making the left and right dynamic branches independently first order. Find current entering A from the inductor and current leaving A to the right, then subtract.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

求换路前的两个状态Find both pre-switch states

S打开稳态时电感短路、电容开路。25 V通过5 Ω、10 Ω、10 Ω串联，电流1 A，故A=20 V、B=10 V。电容下端是40 V，所以上正下负电容电压为−30 V。With S open in steady state, short the inductor and open the capacitor. The 25 V source drives 1 A through series 5 Ω, 10 Ω and 10 Ω, giving A = 20 V and B = 10 V. The capacitor lower terminal is at 40 V, so its top-positive voltage is −30 V.

iL(0+)=1A,uC(0+)=−30V,vB(0+)=10V

求左RL支路Solve the left RL branch

取电感电流由左向右流入A。换路后A=10 V，25 V与10 V之差15 V作用在5 Ω、0.5 H上。最终电流3 A，时间常数0.1 s。Reference inductor current leftward-to-right into A. With A = 10 V, 15 V drives 5 Ω and 0.5 H. The final current is 3 A and time constant 0.1 s.

τL=0.55=0.1s,iL(t)=3−2e−10t(A)

求右RC节点电压Solve the right RC node voltage

稳态时B在两个10 Ω间分得5 V，初值10 V；电容看入电阻10 Ω∥10 Ω=5 Ω，时间常数5×0.2=1 s。40 V下端源为恒定值，所以B与电容电压具有相同时间常数。B approaches 5 V from its 10 V initial value through the two 10 Ω resistors. The capacitor sees 10 Ω∥10 Ω = 5 Ω, giving τ = 5×0.2 = 1 s. Its lower terminal remains at 40 V, so B and capacitor voltage share this time constant.

vB(t)=5+5e−t,iright(t)=10−vB10=0.5−0.5e−t

由节点KCL还原所求电流Recover the requested current by KCL

A点流入电感电流，流出右支路与向下i。因此所求i等于左电流减右电流。At A, inductor current enters, while right-branch current and downward i leave. Hence i is left current minus right current.

i(t)=iL−iright=2.5−2e−10t+0.5e−t(A),t≥0

i(t)=2.5−2e^(−10t)+0.5e^(−t) A，t≥0，按图向下为正。i(t) = 2.5−2e^(−10t)+0.5e^(−t) A for t≥0, with downward positive.

易错点Common errors

  • 电容初始电压是10−40=−30 V，不是节点B的10 V。Initial capacitor voltage is 10−40 = −30 V, not B’s 10 V.
  • 所求i不是电感电流；必须扣除流向右侧的电流。The requested i is not the inductor current; subtract current going right.

核验依据Verification

独立原支路方程：0.5diL/dt+5iL=15，和(B−10)/10+B/10+0.2d(B−40)/dt=0，代入两个响应严格成立；节点A的i=iL−(10−B)/10给出同式。初值i(0⁺)=1 A、最终i(∞)=2.5 A。另将所有电阻耗散、25 V/10 V/40 V源吸收功率及电感电容储能变化率按指数系数精确相加，恒等于0。书第336页/PDF345页5-27一致。Independent branch equations are 0.5diL/dt+5iL = 15 and (B−10)/10 + B/10 + 0.2d(B−40)/dt = 0. Both responses satisfy them exactly, and A-node KCL i = iL−(10−B)/10 reproduces the result. Initial i(0⁺) is 1 A and final i is 2.5 A. An exact exponential-coefficient audit also sums all resistor losses, absorbed source powers and storage-energy rates to zero for every t. Answer 5-27 on printed p. 336/PDF p. 345 agrees.

5-29指定作业Assigned已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第143页／PDF第152页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 143 / PDF p. 152. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：5-29Assigned requirement: 5-29

二次换路：第二段从实际换路值重新开始Two switching events: restart from the actual second-switch state

题目原文Transcribed question

题图5-25所示电路中，开关S原来处于端钮1时，电路已经达到稳态，t=0时刻开关S从端钮1接到端钮2，t=2 s时刻开关S又从端钮2接到端钮1，求t≥0时的u(t)。In Fig. 5-25, the circuit is initially in steady state with S at terminal 1. At t = 0, S moves from 1 to 2; at t = 2 s, it returns from 2 to 1. Find u(t) for t≥0.

左5 mA电流源向上注入节点A，A经顶端2 kΩ到输出节点B。A另经2 kΩ到开关端钮1；B另经1 kΩ到端钮2，开关公共端接底部地。B经50 μF电容再经上正下负3 V源接地。电容电压uC上正下负，输出u从B到地上正下负，所以u=uC+3 V。A 5 mA upward source injects node A. Upper 2 kΩ joins A to output B. A also connects through 2 kΩ to switch terminal 1; B through 1 kΩ to terminal 2. The switch common is grounded. B connects to ground through a 50 μF capacitor followed by a top-positive 3 V source. Capacitor voltage uC and output u are top-positive, with u = uC+3 V.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

分段一阶响应；电容电压连续；每次换路重新求时间常数与最终值。Piecewise first-order responses, capacitor-voltage continuity and new final values/time constants at each switch.
输出u不是单独的电容电压，但3 V恒定，所以它也连续。第二段必须从第一段在2 s的实际值开始，并将时间原点移到2 s。Output u is not the capacitor voltage alone, but its 3 V offset is constant, so u is continuous too. Start the second segment from the actual first-segment value at 2 s and shift its time origin to 2 s.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

初始稳态Initial steady state

S在1时，电容开路，顶端2 kΩ无电流，5 mA全流过左竖直2 kΩ，所以A、B均为10 V。电容初始电压为10−3=7 V。With S at 1 and the capacitor open in steady state, upper 2 kΩ carries no current. All 5 mA flows through the left vertical 2 kΩ, giving A = B = 10 V. Initial capacitor voltage is 10−3 = 7 V.

u(0−)=u(0+)=10V

第一段：S在2First segment: S at 2

此时左竖直2 kΩ下端悬空，电流源通过顶端2 kΩ向B送入5 mA。最终u=5 mA×1 kΩ=5 V。源置零开路后电容看到1 kΩ，故τ₁=0.05 s。The left vertical 2 kΩ ends open. The source sends 5 mA through upper 2 kΩ into B. Final u is 5 mA×1 kΩ = 5 V. Opening the zeroed current source leaves a 1 kΩ resistance seen by the capacitor, so τ₁ = 0.05 s.

u(t)=5+5e−20t(V),0≤t≤2

精确保留2 s的状态Retain the exact state at 2 s

两秒相当于40个时间常数，虽已极接近5 V，严格初值仍为5+5e⁻⁴⁰ V。电容电压与输出在换路时都连续。Two seconds is 40 time constants, so u is extremely close to 5 V, but exactly 5+5e^(−40) V. Both capacitor voltage and output remain continuous at switching.

u(2+)=u(2−)=5+5e−40(V)

第二段：S回到1Second segment: S returns to 1

最终u回到10 V。源置零后电容经顶端2 kΩ和左竖直2 kΩ串联放电，τ₂=4 kΩ×50 μF=0.2 s。以t−2为新时间变量，从精确换路值出发。The final output returns to 10 V. After source suppression, the capacitor sees upper 2 kΩ in series with left vertical 2 kΩ, giving τ₂ = 4 kΩ×50 μF = 0.2 s. Use t−2 and the exact switching value.

u(t)=10−5(1−e−40)e−5(t−2)(V),t>2

说明书后近似式Explain the appendix approximation

书后第二段写10−5e⁻⁵⁽ᵗ⁻²⁾ V，相当于把e⁻⁴⁰舍去。它在工程精度下足够准确，但这里保留小项以严格满足连续性。The appendix writes 10−5e^(−5(t−2)) V for the second segment, dropping e^(−40). This is excellent numerically, but the retained term enforces exact continuity.

5e−40≈2.12×10−17V

0≤t≤2 s：u=5+5e^(−20t) V。 t>2 s：u=10−5(1−e^(−40))e^(−5(t−2)) V。 第二段可近似为书后答案10−5e^(−5(t−2)) V。For 0≤t≤2 s: u = 5+5e^(−20t) V. For t>2 s: u = 10−5(1−e^(−40))e^(−5(t−2)) V. The appendix’s second segment, 10−5e^(−5(t−2)) V, is the approximation dropping the tiny residual.

易错点Common errors

  • 第二段指数是t−2，不是t；时间常数从0.05 s变为0.2 s。The second exponent uses t−2, not t; the time constant changes from 0.05 s to 0.2 s.
  • 不能把输出u与电容电压uC混用；二者相差恒定3 V。Do not confuse output u with capacitor voltage uC; they differ by a constant 3 V.

核验依据Verification

独立原方程：第一段50 μF du/dt+u/1000=5 mA；第二段50 μF du/dt+(u−10)/4000=0。两个分段式逐项代入残差均为0，2 s左右极限均为5+5e⁻⁴⁰ V，最终u=10 V。对应电容电压从7 V趋向2 V，再趋向7 V。书第336页/PDF345页5-29第一段完全一致；第二段是舍去5e⁻⁴⁰ V残余的近似，相差最大约2.12×10⁻¹⁷ V，并非第二次换路可令电容跳变。Independent equations: the first segment satisfies 50 μF du/dt + u/1000 = 5 mA; the second satisfies 50 μF du/dt + (u−10)/4000 = 0. Both residuals vanish identically, both limits at 2 s equal 5+5e^(−40) V, and the final output is 10 V. Capacitor voltage goes from 7 V toward 2 V, then toward 7 V. Answer 5-29 on printed p. 336/PDF p. 345 matches the first segment exactly; its second is the approximation dropping the residual 5e^(−40) V, with maximum difference about 2.12×10⁻¹⁷ V. Switching does not permit a capacitor-voltage jump.

5-31指定作业Assigned已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第143页／PDF第152页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 143 / PDF p. 152. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：5-31Assigned requirement: 5-31

阶跃波形：由跳变时刻与方向写函数Step waveforms: encode transition time and direction

题目原文Transcribed question

用单位阶跃函数表示题图5-27所示的3个电压波形。Express the three voltage waveforms in Fig. 5-27 using the unit-step function.

(a) u₁在t<1 s时为0，在t>1 s时为5 V；(b) u₂在t<−1 s时为0，在t>−1 s时为5 V；(c) u₃在t<1 s时为5 V，在t>1 s时为0。图中跳变点单点取值未指定。(a) u₁ is 0 for t<1 s and 5 V for t>1 s. (b) u₂ is 0 for t<−1 s and 5 V for t>−1 s. (c) u₃ is 5 V for t<1 s and 0 for t>1 s. Values exactly at the jumps are unspecified.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

单位阶跃ε(t)；平移；时间反向；下降阶跃。The unit step ε(t), time translation, time reversal and falling steps.
把“什么时候跳、跳多大、向上还是向下”分开判断。ε(t−t₀)在t₀后开启；ε(t₀−t)在t₀前开启。Identify when the jump occurs, its size and its direction separately. ε(t−t₀) is on after t₀; ε(t₀−t) is on before t₀.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

(a) 延迟1 s的上升阶跃(a) Rising step delayed by 1 s

t−1在1 s前为负、1 s后为正，因此乘5 V得到图(a)。t−1 is negative before 1 s and positive afterward; multiplying by 5 V gives waveform (a).

u1(t)=5ε(t−1)V

(b) 提前1 s的上升阶跃(b) Rising step advanced by 1 s

跳变在−1 s，所以函数参数为t−(−1)=t+1。The jump is at −1 s, so the argument is t−(−1) = t+1.

u2(t)=5ε(t+1)V

(c) 1 s后的下降阶跃(c) Falling step at 1 s

在1 s前应开启、之后关闭，因此使用反向参数1−t；也可写5[1−ε(t−1)]，但跳点取值须使用一致约定。It must be on before 1 s and off afterward, so use 1−t. One can also write 5[1−ε(t−1)], with a consistent convention at the jump.

u3(t)=5ε(1−t)V

(a) 5ε(t−1) V；(b) 5ε(t+1) V；(c) 5ε(1−t) V。(a) 5ε(t−1) V; (b) 5ε(t+1) V; (c) 5ε(1−t) V.

易错点Common errors

  • 提前到−1 s的阶跃是t+1，不是t−1。A step advanced to −1 s uses t+1, not t−1.
  • 下降阶跃需要反向或相减，不能直接再用正的5ε(t−1)。A falling step requires reversal or subtraction, not another positive 5ε(t−1).

核验依据Verification

独立区间取值核验：在t=−2、0、2 s代入三式，依次得到(0,0,5)、(0,5,5)、(5,5,0) V，与图的左右平台一致；跳变量分别+5、+5、−5 V。图未规定跳点取值，采用同一ε(0)约定即可。书第336页/PDF345页5-31三式一致。Independent interval checks: at t = −2, 0 and 2 s, the three expressions give (0,0,5), (0,5,5) and (5,5,0) V, matching the plotted plateaus. Jump sizes are +5, +5 and −5 V. The graph does not specify point values at the jumps; use one consistent ε(0) convention. All three expressions match answer 5-31 on printed p. 336/PDF p. 345.

7-6指定作业Assigned已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第186页／PDF第195页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 186 / PDF p. 195. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：7-6Assigned requirement: 7-6

有效值相量：先统一余弦形式，再除以√2RMS phasors: convert to cosine, then divide by √2

题目原文Transcribed question

写出下列正弦量的有效值相量。 (1) u₁(t)=cosωt；(2) u₂(t)=−sinωt；(3) u₃(t)=−cosωt；(4) u₄(t)=sinωt。Write the RMS phasors of the following sinusoidal quantities: (1) u₁(t) = cosωt; (2) u₂(t) = −sinωt; (3) u₃(t) = −cosωt; (4) u₄(t) = sinωt.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

余弦基准有效值相量；正弦与余弦相差90°；负号等价于180°相移。Cosine-reference RMS phasors, the 90° sine/cosine shift and the 180° phase reversal for a negative sign.
本书采用余弦基准。每个波形幅值为1，故有效值均为1/√2。相量的相角必须从化为正幅值余弦后的初相角读取。The book uses a cosine reference. All amplitudes are 1, so every RMS magnitude is 1/√2. Read the phase after converting to a positive-amplitude cosine.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

统一变换规则Use one consistent conversion rule

若u=Uₘcos(ωt+φ)，有效值相量为(Uₘ/√2)∠φ。不要把相量幅值仍写成原波形的峰值。For u = Uₘcos(ωt+φ), the RMS phasor is (Uₘ/√2)∠φ. Do not retain the waveform’s peak magnitude in an RMS phasor.

u=Umcos(ωt+φ) ⟺ U˙=Um2∠φ

(1)、(3)处理余弦及负号(1), (3): cosine and its negative

cosωt的相角为0°；−cosωt=cos(ωt+180°)。负号进入相角，有效值大小保持非负。cosωt has phase 0°. Also −cosωt = cos(ωt+180°). Move the negative sign into the phase and keep the RMS magnitude nonnegative.

U˙1=12∠0∘,U˙3=12∠180∘

(2)、(4)处理正弦(2), (4): sine waveforms

−sinωt=cos(ωt+90°)，sinωt=cos(ωt−90°)。所以负正弦对应+90°，正正弦对应−90°。−sinωt = cos(ωt+90°) and sinωt = cos(ωt−90°). Thus negative sine gives +90° and positive sine gives −90°.

U˙2=12∠90∘,U˙4=12∠(−90∘)

(1) (1/√2)∠0° V；(2) (1/√2)∠90° V；(3) (1/√2)∠180° V；(4) (1/√2)∠−90° V。1/√2≈0.707。(1) (1/√2)∠0° V; (2) (1/√2)∠90° V; (3) (1/√2)∠180° V; (4) (1/√2)∠−90° V, with 1/√2≈0.707.

易错点Common errors

  • 使用余弦基准时sinωt对应−90°，不是+90°。Under a cosine reference, sinωt has phase −90°, not +90°.
  • 有效值相量的模为1/√2，不是1。An RMS phasor has magnitude 1/√2 here, not 1.

核验依据Verification

独立逆变换核验：将四个相量分别代入u(t)=√2 Re{U̇eʲωᵗ}，还原为cosωt、−sinωt、−cosωt、sinωt；多组时间点数值核验相符。各相量模平方均为1/2，与单位幅值正弦波的周期均方值相同。书第337页/PDF346页7-6给出的0.707及四个相角一致。Independent inverse-transform check: u(t) = √2 Re{U̇e^(jωt)} reconstructs cosωt, −sinωt, −cosωt and sinωt. Numerical checks at multiple times agree. Every squared magnitude is 1/2, the cycle mean-square value of a unit-amplitude sinusoid. The 0.707 magnitudes and all four angles match answer 7-6 on printed p. 337/PDF p. 346.

7-12指定作业Assigned已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第187页／PDF第196页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 187 / PDF p. 196. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：7-12Assigned requirement: 7-12

并联RC电流正交：有效值用勾股关系Orthogonal parallel-RC currents: use the RMS right triangle

题目原文Transcribed question

题图7-6所示正弦交流电路中，已知I=10 A，I₂=6 A，求电流I₁。For the sinusoidal circuit in Fig. 7-6, I = 10 A and I₂ = 6 A are RMS magnitudes. Find I₁.

输入电流I向右，经串联jωL后进入并联电容与电阻R。电容阻抗−j/(ωC)，支路电流I₁向下；电阻支路电流I₂向下。下方为公共返回线。Input current I goes right through series jωL, then splits into parallel capacitor and resistor R. The capacitor impedance is −j/(ωC), with I₁ downward. The resistor branch has I₂ downward. The bottom is the common return.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

相量KCL；并联共电压；电容电流超前电压90°，电阻电流同相。Phasor KCL, common parallel voltage, capacitor current leading by 90° and in-phase resistor current.
电容支路和电阻支路共有电压，两个电流相差90°。因此10 A不是两个支路有效值的算术和，必须对正交相量求模。The capacitor and resistor share a voltage, so their currents are 90° apart. The 10 A total is not the arithmetic sum of branch RMS magnitudes; take the magnitude of orthogonal phasors.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

选择方便的相位基准Choose a convenient phase reference

取并联两端电压为0°基准。电阻电流I₂=6∠0° A，电容电流I₁=jI₁。上游串联电感不改变这两个并联支路电流之间的90°关系。Choose the parallel voltage as the 0° reference. The resistor current is 6∠0° A and capacitor current is jI₁. The upstream series inductor does not change the 90° relation between the two parallel currents.

I˙2=6,I˙1=jI1,I˙=6+jI1

用总电流的模求支路有效值Use the total magnitude to recover the branch RMS value

相量实部、虚部正交，所以总电流有效值平方为两支路平方和。Real and imaginary components are orthogonal, so the squared total RMS magnitude is the sum of the squared branch magnitudes.

102=62+I12⇒I1=100−36=8A

区分相对相角与绝对相角Distinguish relative and absolute phase

在所选并联电压基准下，可写İ₁=j8 A、İ=6+j8 A。题目未提供全电路的绝对相位，确定的数值答案是I₁的有效值8 A。Relative to the chosen parallel-voltage reference, İ₁ = j8 A and İ = 6+j8 A. The problem gives no overall absolute phase; the determined numerical answer is the RMS magnitude I₁ = 8 A.

I˙=10∠53.13∘(relative to the parallel voltage)

I₁=8 A（有效值）。取并联电压为0°时，İ₁=8∠90° A。I₁ = 8 A RMS. With parallel voltage chosen as 0°, İ₁ = 8∠90° A.

易错点Common errors

  • 不能写I₁=10−6=4 A；只有相量而非模满足直接相加。Do not write I₁ = 10−6 = 4 A; phasors, not their magnitudes, add directly.
  • 题目未给绝对相位，不能把自选参考角当作已知绝对相角。Do not present a chosen reference phase as an externally given absolute phase.

核验依据Verification

独立复数核验：6+j8的模为√(36+64)=10，电阻与电容电流的均方交叉项2Re{6·(j8)*}=0，所以总均方值为100 A²。相量KCL与周期均方关系独立吻合。书第337页/PDF346页7-12为8 A。Independent complex check: |6+j8| = √(36+64) = 10. The RMS cross term is 2Re{6·(j8)*} = 0, so the total mean square is 100 A². Phasor KCL and the cycle mean-square relation agree independently. Answer 7-12 on printed p. 337/PDF p. 346 is 8 A.

7-17指定作业Assigned已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第187页／PDF第196页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 187 / PDF p. 196. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：7-17Assigned requirement: 7-17

串联未知元件：相量相减后求阻抗An unknown series element: subtract phasor voltages, then divide

题目原文Transcribed question

题图7-11所示正弦交流电路中，已知U̇=10∠53.1° V，U̇₁=6∠0° V，求İ、U̇₂及未知元件的复阻抗Z₂，并说明该元件是什么元件。For the sinusoidal circuit in Fig. 7-11, U̇ = 10∠53.1° V and U̇₁ = 6∠0° V. Find İ, U̇₂ and the unknown element impedance Z₂, and identify the element.

左电压源U̇上正下负，串联上方3 Ω电阻与右侧Z₂。电流İ顺时针；电阻电压U̇₁左正右负，Z₂电压U̇₂上正下负，均为关联参考方向。The left source U̇ is positive at the top and drives series upper 3 Ω and right Z₂. Current İ is clockwise. U̇₁ across 3 Ω is positive on the left; U̇₂ across Z₂ is positive at the top. Both use passive references.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

有效值相量；串联KVL；矩形式复数相减；阻抗虚部判别。RMS phasors, series KVL, rectangular complex subtraction and impedance classification by reactance.
电阻电压先给出电流相量。再把总电压化为矩形式与6 V相减，最后除以电流。给定53.1°是6−8−10三角形角度53.130…°的一位小数近似。The resistor voltage directly gives the current phasor. Convert total voltage to rectangular form, subtract 6 V, then divide by the current. The given 53.1° is the one-decimal approximation to the 6−8−10 triangle angle 53.130…°.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

由电阻电压求电流Find current from the resistor voltage

3 Ω为纯电阻，电流与U̇₁同相，大小为6/3。The 3 Ω resistor gives current in phase with U̇₁ and magnitude 6/3.

I˙=U˙13=2∠0∘A

矩形式做KVL相减Use rectangular KVL subtraction

按题目相角精度，10∠53.1°≈6+j8 V。因此U̇₂≈(6+j8)−6=j8 V；不是10−6=4 V。To the precision of the given angle, 10∠53.1°≈6+j8 V. Thus U̇₂≈(6+j8)−6 = j8 V, not the scalar difference 10−6 = 4 V.

U˙2=U˙−U˙1≈j8=8∠90∘V

求阻抗并识别元件Find impedance and identify the element

U̇₂除以İ得到正的纯虚阻抗，按本题精度为电感元件，感抗ωL≈4 Ω；题目未给ω，不能单独求L数值。Dividing U̇₂ by İ gives a positive imaginary impedance. To the problem’s precision it is an inductor with ωL≈4 Ω. No numerical L can be found without ω.

Z2=U˙2I˙≈j4Ω,L≈4ωH

保留题目角度舍入的含义Respect the rounded input angle

若把53.1°当作完全精确数，计算得到U̇₂≈0.004202+j7.996847 V，Z₂≈0.002101+j3.998423 Ω。其极小实部来自给定角度舍入；教材标准答案j4 Ω对应理想6−8−10关系。If 53.1° is treated as mathematically exact, U̇₂≈0.004202+j7.996847 V and Z₂≈0.002101+j3.998423 Ω. The tiny real part results from the rounded input angle; the textbook j4 Ω answer uses the ideal 6−8−10 relation.

İ=2∠0° A；U̇₂≈8∠90° V；Z₂≈j4 Ω，为电感元件（按题目相角精度）。İ = 2∠0° A; U̇₂≈8∠90° V; Z₂≈j4 Ω, an inductor to the stated phase precision.

易错点Common errors

  • 相量减法应先化矩形式，不能直接相减电压有效值。Convert to rectangular form for subtraction; do not subtract RMS magnitudes directly.
  • 纯虚阻抗j4 Ω不等于电感4 H；还需角频率。Reactance j4 Ω does not mean an inductance of 4 H; angular frequency is also required.

核验依据Verification

独立回代：İ(3+j4)=2(3+j4)=6+j8，其模为10 V、相角arctan(8/6)=53.130102…°，按一位小数正好53.1°。复功率为U̇İ*=12+j16 VA，3 Ω消耗12 W，电感吸收无功16 var，功率一致。同时保留按53.1°字面计算的微小实部以避免伪称完全精确。书第337页/PDF346页7-17的2∠0°、8∠90°、j4 Ω与此精度解释一致。Independent substitution: İ(3+j4) = 2(3+j4) = 6+j8, with magnitude 10 V and angle arctan(8/6) = 53.130102…°, rounding to 53.1°. Complex power is U̇İ* = 12+j16 VA: 3 Ω consumes 12 W and the inductor absorbs 16 var. The tiny real part obtained from a literal 53.1° calculation is retained explicitly rather than claiming exact equality. Answer 7-17 on printed p. 337/PDF p. 346 gives 2∠0°, 8∠90° and j4 Ω, consistent with this precision.

7-23指定作业Assigned已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第188页／PDF第197页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 188 / PDF p. 197. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：7-23Assigned requirement: 7-23

零支路电流不等于右侧无电流：并联无功相抵Zero branch current can coexist with circulating reactive currents

题目原文Transcribed question

题图7-17所示正弦交流电路中，已知ω=100 rad/s，İ=0，求İ_L₁、İ_C和C的值。For the sinusoidal circuit in Fig. 7-17, ω = 100 rad/s and İ = 0. Find İ_L₁, İ_C and C.

100∠0° V源上正下负，顶端经100 Ω到节点A；A经j100 Ω到地，I_L₁向下；A经200 Ω到节点B，该支路I向右。B经j500 Ω及电容阻抗−j/(ωC)两支路到地，I_L₂和I_C均向下。A top-positive 100∠0° V source connects through 100 Ω to A. A connects through j100 Ω to ground, with I_L₁ downward. A 200 Ω resistor joins A to B, carrying I rightward. B connects through parallel j500 Ω and −j/(ωC) branches to ground, with I_L₂ and I_C downward.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

相量KCL/KVL；零电阻支路电流意味着两端等电位；并联LC导纳相消。Phasor KCL/KVL, equal potentials across a resistor with zero current and cancellation of parallel LC admittances.
İ=0使200 Ω压降为0，故A、B电压相同。右侧电感和电容电流不必分别为0，只需大小相等、方向相反的相量相加为0。İ = 0 makes the 200 Ω voltage drop zero, so A and B have equal voltage. The right inductor and capacitor currents need not vanish individually; equal and opposite phasors can sum to zero.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

由零电流确定右侧导纳Use zero current to determine the right-side admittance

B点KCL为İ_L₂+İ_C=0。B电压非0，因此并联导纳必须为0，电容容抗大小等于500 Ω。KCL at B is İ_L₂+İ_C = 0. Since B’s voltage is nonzero, the parallel admittance must vanish; capacitive reactance magnitude is 500 Ω.

1j500+jωC=0⇒C=1500ω=150000F=20μF

计算左电感电流Calculate the left inductor current

右侧不取净电流，左源只驱动100 Ω与j100 Ω串联，所以电流就是İ_L₁。The right side draws zero net current, so the source drives series 100 Ω and j100 Ω; their current is İ_L₁.

I˙L1=100100+j100=12−j12=22∠(−45∘)A

求公共节点电压与电容电流Find common voltage and capacitor current

A、B电压均为j100İ_L₁=50+j50 V。电容阻抗−j500 Ω，因此İ_C=U̇_B/(−j500)。A and B are both at j100İ_L₁ = 50+j50 V. The capacitor impedance is −j500 Ω, so İ_C = U̇_B/(−j500).

U˙A=U˙B=50+j50,I˙C=−0.1+j0.1=210∠135∘A

İ_L₁=(√2/2)∠−45° A；İ_C=(√2/10)∠135° A；C=20 μF。İ_L₁ = (√2/2)∠−45° A; İ_C = (√2/10)∠135° A; C = 20 μF.

易错点Common errors

  • İ=0不意味着İ_L₂=İ_C=0；它们相量相消。İ = 0 does not imply İ_L₂ = İ_C = 0; their phasors cancel.
  • 电容电流是电压除以−jX，即相位超前90°。Dividing by capacitive −jX makes current lead voltage by 90°.

核验依据Verification

独立完整双节点方程求解（保留200 Ω支路）得到A=B=50+j50 V，故200 Ω电流严格为0；İ_L₂=0.1−j0.1 A，恰与İ_C相反。复功率核验：100 Ω耗散50 W；左电感+50 var，右电感+10 var，电容−10 var；源提供50+j50 VA，总和为0。书第337页/PDF346页7-23的两个相量与20 μF均一致。An independent full two-node solve, retaining the 200 Ω branch, gives A = B = 50+j50 V and zero 200 Ω current. İ_L₂ = 0.1−j0.1 A exactly cancels İ_C. Complex-power check: 100 Ω dissipates 50 W; the left and right inductors absorb +50 and +10 var, and the capacitor −10 var. The source supplies 50+j50 VA, balancing all loads. Both phasors and 20 μF match answer 7-23 on printed p. 337/PDF p. 346.

7-24指定作业Assigned已抄题并核验解析Transcribed & solution checked

来源：电路分析基础，第3版，俎云霄等 · 书第188页／PDF第197页。范围状态：指定作业（指定小问优先）。Source: Fundamentals of Circuit Analysis, 3rd ed. · printed p. 188 / PDF p. 197. Coverage: assigned exercise; stated subparts take priority.

指定要求：7-24Assigned requirement: 7-24

跨节点电压源：用超节点KCL反求源相量A voltage source between nodes: recover its phasor with supernode KCL

题目原文Transcribed question

题图7-18所示正弦交流电路中，已知U̇₁=4∠0° V，试求U̇ₛ。For the sinusoidal circuit in Fig. 7-18, U̇₁ = 4∠0° V. Find U̇ₛ.

底部公共节点。左中节点A经2 Ω接地，电压U₁上正下负；A还经−j1 Ω电容、再经左侧1 Ω接地。A到右中节点B之间有电压源Uₛ，左负右正。B经−j1 Ω电容接地，并经顶端j1 Ω、再经右侧2 Ω串联接地。The bottom is common. Left-middle A connects through 2 Ω to ground, with U₁ positive at A. Another A-to-ground path contains −j1 Ω then left 1 Ω. Voltage source Uₛ lies between A and right-middle B, negative at A and positive at B. B connects to ground through −j1 Ω, and through upper j1 Ω in series with right 2 Ω.

先想：怎么识别与起步Hint: recognition and first step

前置知识Prerequisites

相量节点法；电压源超节点；复数导纳；源电压极性。Phasor nodal analysis, voltage-source supernodes, complex admittances and source polarity.
U̇₁已给出A=4 V。把A、B及它们之间的电压源看成超节点，直接对所有向地支路列KCL，避开未知源电流；最后按右正左负求Uₛ=B−A。U̇₁ fixes A = 4 V. Treat A, B and their intervening voltage source as a supernode, summing all currents to ground without the unknown source current. Finally use the right-positive polarity: Uₛ = B−A.
完整解析与结果核验Full solution & verification

把两边支路写成导纳Write each side’s admittance

A对地有2 Ω及(1−j) Ω两条支路；B对地有−j Ω及(2+j) Ω两条支路。各串联路径之间没有分叉，可先相加阻抗。A has paths of 2 Ω and (1−j) Ω to ground. B has paths of −j Ω and (2+j) Ω. Each series path has no intermediate branch, so its impedances add.

YA=12+11−j=1+j2,YB=1−j+12+j=25+j45

对超节点列KCLApply supernode KCL

没有电流源向超节点外部注入，全部向地电流相加为0。A=4∠0°=4 V，于是可直接解B电位。No current source injects from outside the supernode. Sum all ground-directed currents to zero and use A = 4 V to solve B.

YAU˙A+YBU˙B=0⇒U˙B=−4+j20.4+j0.8=−4+j3(V)

按源极性计算UₛCalculate Uₛ with its stated polarity

图中Uₛ右正左负，源电压是B相对A的电压，不能反过来相减。Uₛ is positive on the right and negative on the left, so it is B relative to A, not the reverse.

U˙s=U˙B−U˙A=(−4+j3)−4=−8+j3V

U̇ₛ=−8+j3 V（图示右正左负）。U̇ₛ = −8+j3 V, positive on the right as drawn.

易错点Common errors

  • B电位−4+j3 V还不是所求源电压；还需减去A=4 V。B’s potential −4+j3 V is not the source voltage; subtract A = 4 V.
  • 电容阻抗−j的导纳为+j，不能漏掉符号变化。The admittance of capacitive impedance −j is +j; retain this sign change.

核验依据Verification

独立完整MNA：将−8+j3 V源接回原电路，解得A=4 V、B=−4+j3 V，左最外节点2+j2 V、右最外节点−2+j4 V，确实重现题设。源正端流入电流4+j2 A，吸收复功率(−8+j3)(4−j2)=−26+j28 VA；三只电阻共耗散26 W，电感与两只电容净无功−28 var，总复功率为0。书第337页/PDF346页7-24为−8+j3 V。Independent full MNA: restoring the −8+j3 V source to the original circuit gives A = 4 V and B = −4+j3 V, with outer-left node 2+j2 V and outer-right node −2+j4 V, reproducing the given voltage. Current entering the source’s positive terminal is 4+j2 A, so its absorbed complex power is (−8+j3)(4−j2) = −26+j28 VA. The three resistors dissipate 26 W and the inductor/capacitors have net reactive power −28 var, giving zero total complex power. Answer 7-24 on printed p. 337/PDF p. 346 is −8+j3 V.
